\sqrt{a+b}\leq\sqrt{a}+\sqrt{b}
and, by the concavity of the square-root function,
\sqrt{a+b}\;=\;\sqrt{\frac{1}{2}(2a)+\frac{1}{2}(2b)}\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(\sqrt{a}+\sqrt{b})
.

Case 4:
\gamma=\omega(\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2})
and
\gamma=\Theta(d^{1/2})
.

We can directly make use of the computations from Case 2 and 3 except that we control
(1-\alpha)^{d/2}
and
(1+\beta)^{d/2}
differently. Since

\displaystyle 0\;\leq\;(1-\alpha)^{d/2}\;=\;(1-\frac{1}{(2+\gamma)^{2}})^{d/2}\;\leq\;1\;,

we see that
A=(A_{1}+A_{2})-(1-(1-\alpha)^{d/2})A_{2}
takes value between
A_{1}+A_{2}
and
A_{1}
, whose Taylor expansions under
\gamma=\omega(\|\mu\|_{2}^{2})
have been obtained in (
33
) and (
27
) respectively. Therefore,

\displaystyle A\;=\;\Theta\Big((A_{1}+A_{2})+A_{1}\Big)\;=\;\Theta\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\|\mu\|_2^4}{\gamma^2}$}}\Big)\;.

To compute
B
, we first recall the Taylor expansion from (
26
) using
\gamma=\omega(1)
and additionally make use of
\gamma=\Theta(d^{1/2})
to get

\displaystyle(1+\beta)^{d/2}\;=\;\exp\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{3d}{2\gamma^2}$}}+o\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{ \gamma^{2}}$}}\right)\Big)\;=\;\exp\big(\Theta(1)\big)\;=\;O(1)\;.

By using the expressions from (
34
), (
35
) and (
36
), we get that

\displaystyle B\;=

\displaystyle\;(B_{1}-B_{2}-B_{3})+((1+\beta)^{d/2}-1)B_{1}+(1-(1-\alpha)^{d/2})B_{3}

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 \|\mu\|_2^2}{(4+\gamma)(2+\gamma)}$}}+o\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\|\bmu\|_2^2}{\gamma^2}$}}\Big)+((1+\beta)^{d/2}-1)(2+o(1))+(1-(1-\alpha)^{d/2})(-2+o(1))

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\Theta\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\|\bmu\|_2^2}{\gamma^2}$}}+((1+\beta)^{d/2}+(1-\alpha)^{d/2}-2)\Big)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;O(\gamma^{-2}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}+1)\;=\;O(1)\;.

In the last equality, we have noted that
\gamma^{-2}\|\mu\|_{2}^{2}=o(\gamma^{-1})=o(1)
by assumption. By additionally noting that
\gamma=\Theta(d^{1/2})
, the variance ratio can therefore be bounded as

\displaystyle\rho_{d}=\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\sigfull}{\sigcond}$}}=\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\sqrt{B}}{\sqrt{A}}$}}=O\Big(\,\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{ 1 }{ \gamma^{-1} \|\mu\|_2^2 }$}}\,\Big)=O\Big(\,\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{ d^{1/2} }{ \|\mu\|_2^2 }$}}\,\Big)\;.

This completes the proof.

Appendix F
Proofs for
Appendix

A

F.1
Proofs for RBF decomposition and verifying
2

In this section, we prove Lemma
20
, Lemma
21
and Lemma
23
.

Proof of Lemma
20

We first focus on the one-dimensional RBF kernel, denoted as
\kappa_{1}
, which can be expressed for
x,x^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}
as

\displaystyle|\kappa_{1}(x,x^{\prime})|\;=\;\big|\exp(-(x-x^{\prime})^{2}/(2\gamma))\big|\;=\;\Big|\exp\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{ x x'}{\gamma}$}}\Big)e^{-x^{2}/(2\gamma)}e^{-(x^{\prime})^{2}/(2\gamma)}\Big|\;.

By applying a Taylor expansion around
0
to the infinitely differentiable function
z\mapsto\exp(\frac{z}{\gamma})
for
z\in\mathbb{R}
, we obtain that for any
K\in\mathbb{N}
and every
x,x^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}
.

\displaystyle\Big|\kappa_{1}(x,x^{\prime})-

\displaystyle\msum_{k=0}^{K}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k!}$}}\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{x x'}{\gamma}$}}\Big)^{k}e^{-x^{2}/(2\gamma)}e^{-(x^{\prime})^{2}/(2\gamma)}\Big|

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\sup\nolimits_{z\in[0,xx^{\prime}]}\Big|\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{(K+1)!}$}}\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{x x'}{\gamma}$}}\Big)^{K+1}e^{z/\gamma}\Big|\;e^{-x^{2}/(2\gamma)}e^{-(x^{\prime})^{2}/(2\gamma)}\;.

Fix
\nu\in(2,4]
. Consider two independent normal random variables
U\sim\mathcal{N}(b_{1},1)
and
V\sim\mathcal{N}(b_{2},1)
for some
b_{1},b_{2}\in\mathbb{R}
, and recall that
\phi^{*}_{k}(x)\coloneqq x^{k}e^{-x^{2}/(2\gamma)}
and
\lambda^{*}_{k}\coloneqq\frac{1}{k!\,\gamma^{k}}
. The above then implies that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big|\kappa_{1}(U,V)-\msum_{k=0}^{K}\lambda^{*}_{k}

\displaystyle\phi^{*}_{k}(U)\phi^{*}_{k}(V)\Big|^{\nu}\Big]

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\sup\nolimits_{z\in[0,UV]}\Big|\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{(K+1)!}$}}\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{UV}{\gamma}$}}\Big)^{K+1}e^{z/\gamma}\Big|^{\nu}\;e^{-\nu U^{2}/(2\gamma)}e^{-\nu V^{2}/(2\gamma)}\Big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{((K+1)! \; \gamma^{K+1})^\nu}$}}\,\mathbb{E}\big[|UV|^{\nu(K+1)}e^{-\nu U^{2}/(2\gamma)-\nu V^{2}/(2\gamma)+\sup\nolimits_{z\in[0,UV]}\nu z/\gamma}\big]

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{((K+1)! \; \gamma^{K+1})^\nu}$}}\,\mathbb{E}\big[|UV|^{\nu(K+1)}e^{-\nu(|U|-|V|)^{2}/(2\gamma)}\big]

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{((K+1)! \; \gamma^{K+1})^\nu}$}}\,\mathbb{E}\big[|U|^{\nu(K+1)}\big]\;\mathbb{E}\big[|V|^{\nu(K+1)}\big]\;.

In the last inequality, we have noted that
U
and
V
are independent and bounded the exponential term from above by
1
. By the formula of absolute moments of a Gaussian, we get that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[|U-b_{1}|^{\nu(K+1)}\big]\;=\;\mathbb{E}\big[|V-b_{2}|^{\nu(K+1)}\big]\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2^{(\nu K)/2}}{\sqrt{\pi}}$}}\Gamma\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\nu K+1}{2}$}}\Big)\;.

By a Jensen’s inequality applied to the convex function
x\mapsto|x|^{\nu(K+1)}
, we get that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[

\displaystyle|U|^{\nu(K+1)}\big]\;=\;\mathbb{E}\big[|b_{1}+(U-b_{1})|^{\nu(K+1)}\big]\;=\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big|\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2}$}}(2b_{1})+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2}$}}(2(U-b_{1}))\Big|^{\nu(K+1)}\Big]

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;2^{\nu(K+1)-1}\big(b^{\nu(K+1)}+\mathbb{E}[|U-b_{1}|^{\nu(K+1)}]\big)\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(2b_1)^{\nu(K+1)}}{2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2^{\frac{3}{2} \nu(K+1)}}{2 \sqrt{\pi}}$}}\Gamma\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\nu(K+1) + 1}{2}$}}\Big)\;.

Similarly, we get that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[|V|^{\nu(K+1)}\big]\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(2b_2)^{\nu(K+1)}}{2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2^{\frac{3}{2} \nu(K+1)}}{2 \sqrt{\pi}}$}}\Gamma\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\nu(K+1) + 1}{2}$}}\Big)\;.

(37)

Substituting these moment bounds and noting that
(K+1)!=\Gamma(K+2)
, we get that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\bigg|\kappa_{1}(U,V)-\msum_{k=0}^{K}\lambda^{*}_{k}\phi^{*}_{k}(U)

\displaystyle\phi^{*}_{k}(V)\bigg|^{\nu}\bigg]

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{ 1 }{\gamma^{\nu(K+1)} \big(\Gamma(K+2)\big)^\nu}$}}\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(2b_1)^{\nu(K+1)}}{2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2^{\frac{3}{2} \nu(K+1)}}{2 \sqrt{\pi}}$}}\Gamma\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\nu(K+1) + 1}{2}$}}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle\;\times\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(2b_2)^{\nu(K+1)}}{2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2^{\frac{3}{2} \nu(K+1)}}{2 \sqrt{\pi}}$}}\Gamma\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\nu(K+1) + 1}{2}$}}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle\;\eqqcolon

\displaystyle\;T\,(A_{1}+B)(A_{2}+B)\;.

As
K
grows, the dominating terms are the Gamma functions, so we only need to control their ratios. By Stirling’s formula for the gamma function, we have
\Gamma(x)=\sqrt{2\pi}\,x^{x-1/2}e^{-x}\big(1+O(x^{-1})\big)
for
x>0
. This immediately implies that

\displaystyle TA_{1}A_{2}\;=\;\Theta\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{ (4b_1b_2 / \gamma)^{\nu(K+1)} }{(K+2)^{\nu(K+3/2)} e^{-\nu(K+2)}}$}}\Big)\;=\;o(1)\;

as
K\rightarrow\infty
. Meanwhile,

\displaystyle\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\Gamma\big( \frac{\nu(K+1) + 1}{2} \big)}{ \big(\Gamma(K+2)\big)^\nu}$}}\;=

\displaystyle\;\Theta\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{K^{\nu K/2}}{ K^{\nu K}}$}}\Big)\;=\;\Theta\Big(K^{-\nu K/2}\Big)\;,

which implies that

\displaystyle TA_{1}B\;=\;\Theta\Big((4\sqrt{2}b_{1}/\gamma)^{\nu K}K^{-\nu K/2}\Big)\;=\;o(1)\;,

since the dominating term is
K^{-\nu K/2}
. Similarly,
TA_{2}B=o(1)
. On the other hand, another application of Stirling’s formula gives that

\displaystyle\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\big(\Gamma\big( \frac{\nu(K+1) + 1}{2} \big)^2}{ \big(\Gamma(K+2)\big)^\nu}$}}\;=

\displaystyle\;(2\pi)^{-(\nu-2)/2}\,\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{ \big( \frac{\nu(K+1)+1}{2} \big)^{\nu(K+1)} }{ (K+2)^{\nu(K+3/2)} }$}}\,e^{-\nu(K+1)-1+\nu(K+2)}\,\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\big(1+O(K^{-1})\big)^2}{\big( 1 + O(K^{-1}) \big)^\nu}$}}

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\Theta\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{ (\nu/2)^{\nu K} K^{\nu K} }{ K^{\nu(K+3/2)} }$}}\Big)\;=\;\Theta\big((\nu/2)^{\nu K}K^{-3\nu/2}\big)\;.

This implies that

\displaystyle TB^{2}\;=\;\Theta\big((8/\gamma)^{\nu K}(\nu/2)^{\nu K}K^{-3\nu/2}\big)\;=\;\Theta\big((2\nu/\gamma)^{\nu K}K^{-3\nu/2}\big)\;=\;o(1)\;,

where we have recalled that
\nu\leq 4
and used the assumption that
\gamma>8
. In summary, we have proved that for
\nu\in(2,4]
and
any
fixed
b_{1},b_{2}\in\mathbb{R}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big|\kappa_{1}(U,V)-\msum_{k=0}^{K}\lambda^{*}_{k}

\displaystyle\phi^{*}_{k}(U)\phi^{*}_{k}(V)\Big|^{\nu}\Big]\;\leq\;T(A_{1}+B)(A_{2}+B)\;\xrightarrow{K\rightarrow\infty}\;0\;.

To extend this to multiple dimensions, we note that for the vectors
\mathbf{x}=(x_{1},\ldots,x_{d})\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
and
\mathbf{x}^{\prime}=(x_{1},\ldots,x_{d})\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, the multi-dimensional RBF kernel can then be expressed as

\displaystyle\kappa(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime})\;=\;\exp\big(-\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}/(2\gamma)\big)\;=\;\mprod_{l=1}^{d}\exp\big(-(x_{l}-x^{\prime}_{l})^{2}/(2\gamma)\big)\;=\;\mprod_{l=1}^{d}\kappa_{1}(x_{l},x^{\prime}_{l})\;.

Recall that we have defined the independent normal vectors
\mathbf{U}\sim\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{0},I_{d})
and
\mathbf{V}\sim\mathcal{N}(\mu,I_{d})
. Let
U_{1},\ldots,U_{d}
be the coordinates of
\mathbf{U}
and
V_{1},\ldots,V_{d}
be those of
\mathbf{V}
, which are all independent since the covariance matrices are
I_{d}
. For
0\leq l\leq d
and
K\in\mathbb{N}
, define the random quantities

\displaystyle S_{j;K}\;\coloneqq

\displaystyle\;\msum_{k=0}^{K}\lambda^{*}_{k}\phi^{*}_{k}(U_{j})\phi^{*}_{k}(V_{j})

and

\displaystyle W_{l;K}\;\coloneqq

\displaystyle\;\Big(\mprod_{j=1}^{l}\kappa_{1}(U_{j},V_{j})\Big)\Big(\mprod_{j=l+1}^{d}S_{j;K}\Big)\;.

In particular
\kappa(\mathbf{U},\mathbf{V})=W_{d;K}
. Now by expanding a telescoping sum and applying a triangle inequality followed by a Jensen’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[|\kappa(\mathbf{U},\mathbf{V})-W_{0;K}|^{\nu}\big]\;=\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big|\msum_{l=1}^{d}(W_{l;K}-W_{l-1;K})\Big|^{\nu}\Big]

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(\msum_{l=1}^{d}|W_{l;K}-W_{l-1;K}|\Big)^{\nu}\Big]

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;d^{\nu-1}\msum_{l=1}^{d}\mathbb{E}[|W_{l;K}-W_{l-1;K}|^{\nu}]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;d^{\nu-1}\msum_{l=1}^{d}\Big(\mprod_{j=1}^{l-1}\mathbb{E}[|\kappa_{1}(U_{j},V_{j})|^{\nu}]\Big)\mathbb{E}[|\kappa_{1}(U_{l},V_{l})-S_{l;K}|^{\nu}]\Big(\mprod_{j=l+1}^{d}\mathbb{E}[|S_{j;K}|^{\nu}]\Big)\;.

In the last equality, we have used the independence of
U_{j}
’s and
V_{j}
’s. To bound the summands, we first note that
\kappa_{1}
is uniformly bounded in norm by
1
, which implies that
\mathbb{E}[|\kappa_{1}(U_{j},V_{j})|^{\nu}]\leq 1
. By the previous result,
\mathbb{E}[|\kappa_{1}(U_{l},V_{l})-S_{l;K}|^{\nu}]=o(1)
as
K\rightarrow\infty
. By a triangle inequality and a Jensen’s inequality, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[|S_{j;K}|^{\nu}]\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\big[\big||\kappa_{1}(U_{j},V_{j})|+|S_{j;K}-\kappa_{1}(U_{j},V_{j})|\big|^{\nu}\big]

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;2^{\nu-1}\mathbb{E}\big[|\kappa_{1}(U_{j},V_{j})|^{\nu}\big]+2^{\nu-1}\mathbb{E}\big[|S_{j;K}-\kappa_{1}(U_{j},V_{j})|^{\nu}\big]\;\leq\;2^{\nu-1}+o(1)\;.

This implies that each summand satisfies

\displaystyle\Big(\mprod_{j=1}^{l-1}\mathbb{E}[|\kappa_{1}(U_{j},V_{j})|^{\nu}]\Big)\mathbb{E}[|\kappa_{1}(U_{l},V_{l})-S_{l;K}|^{\nu}]\Big(\mprod_{j=l+1}^{d}\mathbb{E}[|S_{j;K}|^{\nu}]\Big)\;=\;o(1)\;

as
K\rightarrow\infty
. Since
d
is not affected by
K
, we have shown the desired result

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big|\kappa(\mathbf{U},\mathbf{V})-\mprod_{j=1}^{d}\Big(\msum_{k=0}^{K}\lambda^{*}_{k}\phi^{*}_{k}(U_{j})\phi^{*}_{k}(V_{j})\Big)\Big|^{\nu}\Big]\;=\;\mathbb{E}\big[|\kappa(\mathbf{U},\mathbf{V})-W_{0;K}|^{\nu}\big]\;\xrightarrow{K\rightarrow\infty}\;0\;.

\jmlrQED

Proof of Lemma
21

We first rewrite
u_{P}^{\rm KSD}
as

\displaystyle u_{P}^{\rm KSD}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime})\;=

\displaystyle\;e^{-\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}/(2\gamma)}\Big(\mathbf{x}^{\top}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma+ 1}{\gamma^2}$}}\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\Big)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;e^{-\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}/(2\gamma)}\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma+1}{\gamma^2}$}}(\|\mathbf{x}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2})+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma^2+2\gamma+2}{\gamma^2}$}}\mathbf{x}^{\top}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\Big)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;e^{-\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}/(2\gamma)}\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma+1}{\gamma^2}$}}(\|\mathbf{x}\|_{2}^{2}+1)(\|\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}+1)+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma+1}{\gamma^2}$}}\|\mathbf{x}\|_{2}^{2}\|\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\;\;\;\qquad\qquad\qquad+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma^2+2\gamma+2}{\gamma^2}$}}\msum_{l=1}^{d}x_{l}x^{\prime}_{l}+\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma+1}{\gamma^2}$}}\Big)\Big)\;.

For
K^{\prime}\in\mathbb{N}
, write
S_{K^{\prime}}\coloneqq\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{K^{\prime}}\alpha_{k^{\prime}}\psi_{k^{\prime}}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\psi_{k^{\prime}}(\mathbf{X}_{2})
, and define the following random variables comparing each set of eigenvalue and eigenfunction to the corresponding term in
u_{P}^{\rm KSD}
:

\displaystyle T_{K^{\prime};1}\;=

\displaystyle\;\msum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{K^{\prime}}\lambda_{(k^{\prime}-1)(d+3)+1}\,\phi_{(k^{\prime}-1)(d+3)+1}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\,\phi_{(k^{\prime}-1)(d+3)+1}(\mathbf{X}_{1})

\displaystyle\;\quad-e^{-\|\mathbf{X}_{1}-\mathbf{X}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}/(2\gamma)}\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma+1}{\gamma^2}$}}(\|\mathbf{X}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+1)(\|\mathbf{X}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}+1)\Big)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma+1}{\gamma^2}$}}(\|\mathbf{X}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+1)(\|\mathbf{X}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}+1)S_{K^{\prime}}\;,

\displaystyle T_{K^{\prime};2}\;=

\displaystyle\;\msum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{K^{\prime}}\lambda_{(k^{\prime}-1)(d+3)+2}\,\phi_{(k^{\prime}-1)(d+3)+2}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\,\phi_{(k^{\prime}-1)(d+3)+2}(\mathbf{X}_{1})

\displaystyle\;\quad-e^{-\|\mathbf{X}_{1}-\mathbf{X}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}/(2\gamma)}\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma+1}{\gamma^2}$}}\|\mathbf{X}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\|\mathbf{X}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma+1}{\gamma^2}$}}\|\mathbf{X}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\|\mathbf{X}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}\,S_{K^{\prime}}\;,

\displaystyle T_{K^{\prime};3}\;=

\displaystyle\;\msum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{K^{\prime}}\lambda_{(k^{\prime}-1)(d+3)+3}\,\phi_{(k^{\prime}-1)(d+3)+3}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\,\phi_{(k^{\prime}-1)(d+3)+3}(\mathbf{X}_{1})

\displaystyle\;\quad-e^{-\|\mathbf{X}_{1}-\mathbf{X}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}/(2\gamma)}\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma+1}{\gamma^2}$}}\Big)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma+1}{\gamma^2}$}}\Big)\,S_{K^{\prime}}\;,

\displaystyle T_{K^{\prime};3+l}\;=

\displaystyle\;\msum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{K^{\prime}}\lambda_{(k^{\prime}-1)(d+3)+3+l}\,\phi_{(k^{\prime}-1)(d+3)+3+l}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\,\phi_{(k^{\prime}-1)(d+3)+3+l}(\mathbf{X}_{1})

\displaystyle\;\quad-e^{-\|\mathbf{X}_{1}-\mathbf{X}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}/(2\gamma)}\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma^2+2\gamma+2}{\gamma^2}$}}(\mathbf{X}_{1})_{l}(\mathbf{X}_{2})_{l}\big)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma^2+2\gamma+2}{\gamma^2}$}}(\mathbf{X}_{1})_{l}(\mathbf{X}_{2})_{l}\Big)\,S_{K^{\prime}}\;

for
l=1,\ldots,d
, where we have denoted the
l
-th coordinates of
\mathbf{X}_{1}
and
\mathbf{X}_{2}
by
(\mathbf{X}_{1})_{l}
and
(\mathbf{X}_{2})_{l}
respectively. We now bound the approximation error with
(d+3)K^{\prime}
summands for
K^{\prime}\in\mathbb{N}
and
\nu\in(2,3]
. Fix some
\nu_{1}\in(\nu,4]
and let
\nu_{2}=1/(\nu^{-1}-\nu_{1}^{-1})
. By using the quantites defined above, a Jensen’s inequality to the convex function
x\mapsto|x|^{\nu}
and a Hölder’s inequality to each
\mathbb{E}[|T_{K^{\prime};l}|^{\nu}]
, we have

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\msum_{k=1}^{(d+3)K^{\prime}}\lambda_{k}\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})-u_{P}^{\rm KSD}(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})\big|^{\nu}\big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\msum_{l=1}^{d+3}T_{K^{\prime};l}\big|^{\nu}\big]

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;(d+3)^{\nu-1}\msum_{l=1}^{d+3}\mathbb{E}[|T_{K^{\prime};l}|^{\nu}]

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;(d+3)^{\nu-1}\mathbb{E}[|S_{K^{\prime}}|^{\nu_{1}}]^{\nu/\nu_{1}}\Big(\;\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma+1}{\gamma^2}$}}\Big)^{\nu}\mathbb{E}[(\|\mathbf{X}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+1)^{\nu_{2}}]^{\nu/\nu_{2}}\;\mathbb{E}[(\|\mathbf{X}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}+1)^{\nu_{2}}]^{\nu/\nu_{2}}

\displaystyle\;\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma+1}{\gamma^2}$}}\Big)^{\nu}\mathbb{E}\big[\|\mathbf{X}_{1}\|_{2}^{2\nu_{2}}\big]^{\nu/\nu_{2}}\mathbb{E}\big[\|\mathbf{X}_{2}\|_{2}^{2\nu_{2}}\big]^{\nu/\nu_{2}}+\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma+1}{\gamma^2}$}}\Big)^{\nu}

\displaystyle\;\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\msum_{l=1}^{d}\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma^2+2\gamma+2}{\gamma^2}$}}\Big)^{\nu}\mathbb{E}\big[|(\mathbf{X}_{1})_{l}|^{\nu_{2}}\big]^{\nu/\nu_{2}}\mathbb{E}\big[|(\mathbf{X}_{2})_{l}|^{\nu_{2}}\big]^{\nu/\nu_{2}}\Big)\;.

The only
K^{\prime}
-dependence above comes from
\mathbb{E}[|S_{K^{\prime}}|^{\nu_{1}}]^{\nu/\nu_{1}}=\|S_{K^{\prime}}\|_{L_{\nu_{1}}}^{\nu}
, which converges to 0 as
K^{\prime}
grows by Lemma
20
. Therefore

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\msum_{k=1}^{(d+3)K^{\prime}}\lambda_{k}\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})-u_{P}^{\rm KSD}(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})\big|^{\nu}\big]\;\xrightarrow{K^{\prime}\rightarrow\infty}0\;.

Now for
K\in\mathbb{N}
not necessarily divisible by
d+3
, we let
K^{\prime}
be the largest integer such that
dK^{\prime}\leq K
. By a triangle inequality and a Jensen’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})-u_{P}^{\rm KSD}(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})\big|^{\nu}\big]

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(\big|\msum_{k=1}^{(d+3)K^{\prime}}\lambda_{k}\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})-u_{P}^{\rm KSD}(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})\big|+\big|\msum_{k=(d+3)K^{\prime}+1}^{K}\lambda_{k}\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})\big|\Big)^{\nu}\Big]

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;2^{\nu-1}\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\msum_{k=1}^{(d+3)K^{\prime}}\lambda_{k}\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})-u_{P}^{\rm KSD}(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})\big|^{\nu}\big]

\displaystyle\;+2^{\nu-1}\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\msum_{k=(d+3)K^{\prime}+1}^{K}\lambda_{k}\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})\big|^{\nu}\big]\;.

The goal is to show that the bound converges to
0
as
K
grows. We have already shown that the first term is
o(1)
, so we focus on the second term. The expectation in the second term can be bounded using a Jensen’s inequality as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\msum_{k=(d+3)K^{\prime}+1}^{K}\lambda_{k}

\displaystyle\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})\big|^{\nu}\big]\;\leq\;\mathbb{E}\big[\big(\msum_{k=(d+3)K^{\prime}+1}^{K}|\lambda_{k}\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})|\big)^{\nu}\big]

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;(K-(d+3)K^{\prime})^{\nu-1}\msum_{k=(d+3)K^{\prime}+1}^{K}\mathbb{E}\big[\big(\lambda_{k}\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})\big)^{\nu}\big]

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;d^{\nu}\sup\nolimits_{k\in\{(d+3)K^{\prime}+1,\ldots,(d+3)K^{\prime}+(d+3)\}}\mathbb{E}\big[\big(\lambda_{k}\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})\big)^{\nu}\big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;d^{\nu}\sup\nolimits_{1\leq l\leq d+3}\mathbb{E}\big[\big(\lambda_{(d+3)K^{\prime}+l}\phi_{(d+3)K^{\prime}+l}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{(d+3)K^{\prime}+l}(\mathbf{X}_{2})\big)^{\nu}\big]\;.

By observing the formula for
\lambda_{k}
and
\phi_{k}
, we see that there exists some
K
-independent constant
C_{d,\gamma}
such that for
1\leq l\leq d+3
,

\displaystyle|\lambda_{(d+3)K^{\prime}+l}|\;

\displaystyle\leq\;C_{d,\gamma}\alpha_{K^{\prime}+1}

and

\displaystyle|\phi_{(d+3)K^{\prime}+l}|\;

\displaystyle\leq\;C_{d,\gamma}\psi_{K^{\prime}+1}(\mathbf{x})(\|\mathbf{x}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\mathbf{x}\|_{2}+1)\;.

This allows us to obtain the bound

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\msum_{k=(d+3)K^{\prime}+1}^{K}\lambda_{k}\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})\big|^{\nu}\big]

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;d^{\nu}C_{d,\gamma}^{2}\alpha_{K^{\prime}+1}^{\nu}\mathbb{E}[(\psi_{K^{\prime}+1}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\psi_{K^{\prime}+1}(\mathbf{X}_{2}))^{\nu}\,(\|\mathbf{X}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\mathbf{X}_{1}\|_{2}+1)^{\nu}(\|\mathbf{X}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\mathbf{X}_{2}\|_{2}+1)^{\nu}]

\displaystyle\;\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle\;d^{\nu}C^{\prime}_{d,\gamma}\Big(\mprod_{l=1}^{d}\lambda^{*}_{[g_{d}(K^{\prime}+1)]_{l}}\Big)^{\nu}\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(\mprod_{l=1}^{d}\phi^{*}_{[g_{d}(K^{\prime}+1)]_{l}}\big((\mathbf{X}_{1})_{l}\big)\;\phi^{*}_{[g_{d}(K^{\prime}+1)]_{l}}\big((\mathbf{X}_{2})_{l})\big)\Big)^{\nu}

\displaystyle\;\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times(\|\mathbf{X}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\mathbf{X}_{1}\|_{2}+1)^{\nu}(\|\mathbf{X}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\mathbf{X}_{2}\|_{2}+1)^{\nu}\Big]

\displaystyle\;\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\;d^{\nu}C^{\prime}_{d,\gamma}\mathbb{E}\big[(\|\mathbf{X}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\mathbf{X}_{1}\|_{2}+1)^{2\nu}(\|\mathbf{X}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\mathbf{X}_{2}\|_{2}+1)^{2\nu}\big]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\;\;\times\Big(\mprod_{l=1}^{d}\lambda^{*}_{[g_{d}(K^{\prime}+1)]_{l}}\Big)^{\nu}\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(\mprod_{l=1}^{d}\phi^{*}_{[g_{d}(K^{\prime}+1)]_{l}}\big((\mathbf{X}_{1})_{l}\big)\;\phi^{*}_{[g_{d}(K^{\prime}+1)]_{l}}\big((\mathbf{X}_{2})_{l})\big)\Big)^{2\nu}\Big]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\;\overset{(c)}{=}

\displaystyle\;d^{\nu}C^{\prime}_{d,\gamma}\mathbb{E}\big[(\|\mathbf{X}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\mathbf{X}_{1}\|_{2}+1)^{2\nu}(\|\mathbf{X}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\mathbf{X}_{2}\|_{2}+1)^{2\nu}\big]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\;\;\times\mprod_{l=1}^{d}\big(\lambda^{*}_{[g_{d}(K^{\prime}+1)]_{l}}\big)^{\nu}\;\Big(\mprod_{l=1}^{d}\mathbb{E}\big[\big(\phi^{*}_{[g_{d}(K^{\prime}+1)]_{l}}\big((\mathbf{X}_{1})_{l}\big)\,\big)^{2\nu}\big]\mathbb{E}\big[\big(\phi^{*}_{[g_{d}(K^{\prime}+1)]_{l}}\big((\mathbf{X}_{2})_{l}\big)\,\big)^{2\nu}\big]\Big)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\;\overset{(d)}{=}

\displaystyle\;d^{\nu}C^{\prime}_{d,\gamma}\mathbb{E}\big[(\|\mathbf{X}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\mathbf{X}_{1}\|_{2}+1)^{2\nu}(\|\mathbf{X}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\mathbf{X}_{2}\|_{2}+1)^{2\nu}\big]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\;\;\times\mprod_{l=1}^{d}\Big(\big(\lambda^{*}_{[g_{d}(K^{\prime}+1)]_{l}}\big)^{\nu}\;\mathbb{E}\big[\big(\phi^{*}_{[g_{d}(K^{\prime}+1)]_{l}}\big((\mathbf{X}_{1})_{l}\big)\,\big)^{2\nu}\big]\Big)\;,

where we have used the definitions of
\alpha_{k}
and
\psi_{k}
from (
7
) in
(a)
, a Cauchy-Schwarz inequality in
(b)
, the independence of
(\mathbf{X}_{1})_{l}
and
(\mathbf{X}_{2})_{l}
for
1\leq l\leq d
due to the identity covariance matrix in
(c)
and finally the fact that
\mathbf{X}_{1}
and
\mathbf{X}_{2}
are identically distributed in
(d)
. The only quantity that depends on
K^{\prime}
now is

\displaystyle\big(\lambda^{*}_{[g_{d}(K^{\prime}+1)]_{l}}\big)^{\nu}\;\mathbb{E}\big[\big(\phi^{*}_{[g_{d}(K^{\prime}+1)]_{l}}\big((\mathbf{X}_{1})_{l}\big)\,\big)^{2\nu}\big]

for
1\leq l\leq d
. We now seek to bound this quantity. Recall from Lemma
20
that
\lambda^{*}_{k}\coloneqq\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k! \, \gamma^k}$}}
, and for
V\sim\mathcal{N}(b,1)
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[(\phi^{*}_{k}(U))^{2\nu}]\;=\;\mathbb{E}\big[|U|^{2\nu k}e^{-\nu U^{2}/\gamma}\big]\;\leq\;\mathbb{E}\big[|U|^{2\nu k}\big]\;\leq\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(2b)^{2\nu k}}{2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2^{3\nu k}}{2 \sqrt{\pi}}$}}\,\Gamma\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 \nu k +1}{2}$}}\Big)\;.

where we have used a bound similar to (
37
) in the proof of Lemma
20
. By Stirling’s formula for the gamma function, we have
\Gamma(x)=\sqrt{2\pi}\,x^{x-1/2}e^{-x}\big(1+O(x^{-1})\big)
for
x>0
, which implies

\displaystyle(\lambda^{*}_{k})^{\nu}\,\mathbb{E}[(\phi^{*}_{k}(U))^{2\nu}]\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{ (k!)^\nu\, \gamma^{\nu k}}$}}\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(2b)^{2\nu k}}{2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2^{3\nu k}}{2 \sqrt{\pi}}$}}\,\Gamma\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 \nu k +1}{2}$}}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;O\Big(\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{8}{\gamma}$}}\Big)^{\nu k}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(\nu k)^{\nu k} e^{- \nu k} }{(k+1)^{\nu(k+1/2)} e^{-\nu(k+1)}}$}}\Big)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;O\Big(\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{8 \nu}{\gamma}$}}\Big)^{\nu k}\Big)\;=\;O\Big(\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{24}{\gamma}$}}\Big)^{\nu k}\Big)\;=\;o(1)\;

as
k\rightarrow\infty
, where we have used the assumption that
\gamma>24
. By construction of
g_{d}
in (
7
), as
K^{\prime}\rightarrow\infty
,
\min_{1\leq l\leq d}[g_{d}(K^{\prime}+1)]_{l}\rightarrow\infty
, which implies that

\displaystyle\big(\lambda^{*}_{[g_{d}(K^{\prime}+1)]_{l}}\big)^{\nu}\;\mathbb{E}\big[\big(\phi^{*}_{[g_{d}(K^{\prime}+1)]_{l}}\big((\mathbf{X}_{1})_{l}\big)\,\big)^{2\nu}\big]\;\xrightarrow{K^{\prime}\rightarrow\infty}\;0\;.

Therefore

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\msum_{k=(d+3)K^{\prime}+1}^{K}\lambda_{k}\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})\big|^{\nu}\big]\;\xrightarrow{K\rightarrow\infty}\;0\;,

which finishes the proof that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})-u_{P}^{\rm KSD}(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})\big|^{\nu}\big]\;\xrightarrow{K\rightarrow\infty}\;0\;.

In other words,
2
holds.

\jmlrQED

Proof of Lemma
23

Fix
\nu\in(2,3]
. Consider the independent Gaussian vectors
\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2}\overset{i.i.d.}{\sim}P\equiv\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{0},I_{d})
and
\mathbf{Y}_{1},\mathbf{Y}_{2}\overset{i.i.d.}{\sim}Q\equiv\mathcal{N}(\mu,I_{d})
. Write
\mathbf{Z}_{1}=(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{Y}_{1})
,
\mathbf{Z}_{2}=(\mathbf{X}_{2},\mathbf{Y}_{2})
and

\displaystyle T_{K}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime})\;\coloneqq\;e^{-\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}/(2\gamma)}-\msum_{k=1}^{K}\alpha_{k}\psi_{k}(\mathbf{x})\psi_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime})

for
K\in\mathbb{N}
, and recall that

\displaystyle u^{\rm MMD}(\mathbf{Z}_{1},\mathbf{Z}_{2})\;=\;e^{-\|\mathbf{X}_{1}-\mathbf{X}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}/(2\gamma)}-e^{-\|\mathbf{X}_{1}-\mathbf{Y}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}/(2\gamma)}-e^{-\|\mathbf{X}_{2}-\mathbf{Y}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}/(2\gamma)}+e^{-\|\mathbf{Y}_{1}-\mathbf{Y}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}/(2\gamma)}\;.

Then by a triangle inequality and Jensen’s inequality, we get that

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big|u^{\rm MMD}(\mathbf{Z}_{1},\mathbf{Z}_{2})-\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}\phi_{k}(\mathbf{Z}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{Z}_{2})\Big|^{\nu}\Big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big|u^{\rm MMD}(\mathbf{Z}_{1},\mathbf{Z}_{2})-\msum_{k=1}^{K}\alpha_{k}\big(\psi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})-\psi_{k}(\mathbf{Y}_{1})\big)\big(\psi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})-\psi_{k}(\mathbf{Y}_{2})\big)\Big|^{\nu}\Big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\big[\big|T_{K}(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})-T_{K}(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{Y}_{2})-T_{K}(\mathbf{X}_{2},\mathbf{Y}_{1})+T_{K}(\mathbf{X}_{2},\mathbf{Y}_{2})\big|^{\nu}\big]

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;4^{\nu-1}\big(\mathbb{E}[|T_{K}(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})|^{\nu}]+\mathbb{E}[|T_{K}(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{Y}_{2})|^{\nu}]+\mathbb{E}[|T_{K}(\mathbf{X}_{2},\mathbf{Y}_{1})|^{\nu}]+\mathbb{E}[|T_{K}(\mathbf{Y}_{1},\mathbf{Y}_{2})|^{\nu}]\big)\;.

Since each expectation is taken with respect to a product of two Gaussian distributions with identity covariance matrices, by Lemma
20
and (
8
), they all decay to
0
as
K\rightarrow\infty
. This proves that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big|u^{\rm MMD}(\mathbf{Z}_{1},\mathbf{Z}_{2})-\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}\phi_{k}(\mathbf{Z}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{Z}_{2})\Big|^{\nu}\Big]\;\xrightarrow{K\rightarrow\infty}\;0\;,

and therefore
2
holds.

\jmlrQED

F.2
Proof for Lemma
22

We restate the KSD U-statistic for RBF under our Gaussian mean-shift setup from (
9
):

\displaystyle u_{P}^{\rm KSD}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime})\;=

\displaystyle\;\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2\gamma}$}}\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\Big(\mathbf{x}^{\top}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma+ 1}{\gamma^2}$}}\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\Big)\;.

(38)

F.2.1
Proof for
g(\mathbf{x})

Fix
\mathbf{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
. Taking expectation of
u_{P}^{\rm KSD}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{X}^{\prime})
with respect to the distribution of
\mathbf{X}^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle g(\mathbf{x})\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}[u_{P}^{\rm KSD}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{X}^{\prime})]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2\gamma}$}}\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{X}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\left(\mathbf{x}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\prime}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{\gamma^2}$}}\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{X}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\right)\Big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2(1 + \gamma)}$}}\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{x}^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{\gamma^2}$}}\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\right]\;.

where the third line follows by applying Lemma
28
, and
\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\frac{1}{1+\gamma}\big(\mu+\frac{1}{\gamma}\mathbf{x}\big),\frac{\gamma}{1+\gamma}I_{d}\right)
. The proof is completed by calculating the expectation as

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{x}^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{\gamma^2}$}}\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\right]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{x}^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{\gamma^2}$}}\left(\|\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}-2\mathbf{x}^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}+\|\mathbf{x}\|_{2}^{2}\right)+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\right]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\left(\mathbf{\mu}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{\gamma}$}}\mathbf{x}\right)^{\top}\mathbf{x}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma+ 1}{\gamma^2}$}}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma d}{1 + \gamma}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma^2}{(1 + \gamma)^2}$}}\big\|\mathbf{\mu}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{\gamma}$}}\mathbf{x}\big\|_{2}^{2}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\left(\mathbf{\mu}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{\gamma}$}}\mathbf{x}\right)^{\top}\mathbf{x}+\|\mathbf{x}\|_{2}^{2}\right)+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 + \gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{x}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{1 + \gamma}$}}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\;.

F.2.2
Proof for
D^{\rm KSD}(Q,P)

Noting that
D^{\rm KSD}(Q,P)=\mathbb{E}[g^{\rm KSD}(\mathbf{x})]
, we can apply Lemma
28
again to yield

\displaystyle D^{\rm KSD}(Q,P)\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\mathbb{E}\Big[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2(1 + \gamma)}$}}\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 + \gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{X}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{1 + \gamma}$}}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\Big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 + \gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{W}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{1 + \gamma}$}}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\Big]\;,

where
W\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\frac{1+\gamma}{2+\gamma}\big(\mathbf{\mu}+\frac{1}{1+\gamma}\mathbf{\mu}\big),\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}I_{d}\right)
. We then have

\displaystyle D^{\rm KSD}(Q,P)\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\left(\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{\mu}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{1 + \gamma}$}}\mathbf{\mu}\right)-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{1 + \gamma}$}}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\;,

as required.

F.2.3
Proof for
\sigma_{\rm cond}^{2}

We first calculate the second moment as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[g(\mathbf{X})^{2}]\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d}\mathbb{E}\Big[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{1 + \gamma}$}}\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 + \gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{X}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{1 + \gamma}$}}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{3 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\mathbb{E}\Big[\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 + \gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{W}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{1 + \gamma}$}}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{2}\Big]

where in the last line we have applied Lemma
28
while setting
\mathbf{W}\sim\mathcal{N}\big(\mathbf{m},\frac{1+\gamma}{3+\gamma}I_{d}\big)
and
\mathbf{m}\coloneqq\frac{1+\gamma}{3+\gamma}\big(\mathbf{\mu}+\frac{2}{1+\gamma}\mathbf{\mu}\big)=\mathbf{\mu}
. This gives

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[g(\mathbf{X})^{2}]\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{3 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\mathbb{E}\Big[\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 + \gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{2}(\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{W})^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{(1 + \gamma)^2}$}}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{4}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2(2 + \gamma)}{(1 + \gamma)^2}$}}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{W}\Big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{3 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\Big(\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 + \gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{2}\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{3 + \gamma}$}}I_{d}+\mu\mu^{\top}\right)\mathbf{\mu}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{(1 + \gamma)^2}$}}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{4}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2(2 + \gamma)}{(1 + \gamma)^2}$}}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{4}\Big)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma^2}{(1 + \gamma)(3 + \gamma)}$}}\right)^{d/2}\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(2 + \gamma)^2}{(1 + \gamma)(3 + \gamma)}$}}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{4}\Big)\;.

We hence obtain

\displaystyle\sigma_{\rm cond}^{2}\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}[g(\mathbf{X})^{2}]-D^{\rm KSD}(Q,P)^{2}

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma^2}{(1 + \gamma)(3 + \gamma)}$}}\right)^{d/2}\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(2 + \gamma)^2}{(1 + \gamma)(3 + \gamma)}$}}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{4}\Big)-\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{4}

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma^2}{(1 + \gamma)(3 + \gamma)}$}}\right)^{d/2}\bigg(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(2 + \gamma)^2}{(1 + \gamma)(3 + \gamma)}$}}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}+\left(1-\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(1 + \gamma)(3 + \gamma)}{(2 + \gamma)^2}$}}\right)^{d/2}\right)\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{4}\bigg)\;.

F.2.4
Proof for
\sigma_{\rm full}^{2}

For simplicity, we define
\mathbf{Z}\coloneqq\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{\mu}
and
\mathbf{Z}^{\prime}\coloneqq\mathbf{X}^{\prime}z-\mathbf{\mu}
so that
\mathbf{Z},\mathbf{Z}^{\prime}
are independent copies from
\mathcal{N}(0,I_{d})
. By (
38
), the second moment can be simplified as

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}[u_{P}^{\rm KSD}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{X}^{\prime})^{2}]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{\gamma}$}}\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{X}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\Big(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\prime}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma+ 1}{\gamma^2}$}}\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{X}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\Big)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{\gamma}$}}\|\mathbf{Z}-\mathbf{Z}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\Big((\mathbf{Z}+\mathbf{\mu})^{\top}(\mathbf{Z}^{\prime}+\mathbf{\mu})-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma+ 1}{\gamma^2}$}}\|\mathbf{Z}-\mathbf{Z}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\Big)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{4 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\Big((\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu})^{\top}(\mathbf{W}^{\prime}+\alpha_{1}\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu})-\alpha_{2}\|(1-\alpha_{1})\mathbf{W}-\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\Big)^{2}\right]}_{=:T}\;,

where in the last line we have applied Lemma
29
, and

\displaystyle\mathbf{W}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(0,\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma/ 2}{2 + \gamma/ 2}$}}I_{d}\right)\;,\qquad\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(0,\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}I_{d}\right)\;,\qquad\alpha_{1}\;\coloneqq\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{1 + \gamma/ 2}$}}\;,\qquad\alpha_{2}\;\coloneqq\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma+ 1}{\gamma^2}$}}\;.

We now aim to compute the expectation
T
by first taking an expectation over
\mathbf{W}^{\prime}
:

\displaystyle T\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\left[\left((\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu})^{\top}(\mathbf{W}^{\prime}+\alpha_{1}\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu})-\alpha_{2}\|(1-\alpha_{1})\mathbf{W}-\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big(-\alpha_{2}\big\|\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\big\|_{2}^{2}+\big(\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu}+2\alpha_{2}(1-\alpha_{1})\mathbf{W}\big)^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\underbrace{(\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu})^{\top}(\alpha_{1}\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu})-\alpha_{2}(1-\alpha_{1})^{2}\|\mathbf{W}\|_{2}^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}}_{=:\beta_{\mathbf{W}}}\Big)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\alpha_{2}^{2}\big\|\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\big\|_{2}^{4}+\Big((\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu}+2\alpha_{2}(1-\alpha_{1})\mathbf{W})^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\Big)^{2}+\beta_{\mathbf{W}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\;\;-2\alpha_{2}\big(\mathbf{W}+\mu+2\alpha_{2}(1-\alpha_{1})\mathbf{W}\big)^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\big\|\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}-2\alpha_{2}\beta_{\mathbf{W}}\big\|\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\big\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\;\;+2\beta_{\mathbf{W}}(\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu}+2\alpha_{2}(1-\alpha_{1})\mathbf{W})^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\Big]\;.

Since
\mathbf{W}^{\prime}
is zero-mean, independent of
\mathbf{W}
and follows a distribution symmetric around zero,
\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{W}^{\prime}]=\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\|\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}]=\mathbf{0}
. Since
\|\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}\sim\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{2+\gamma}$}}\chi^{2}_{d}
where
\chi^{2}_{d}
is a chi-squared distribution with
d
degrees of freedom, we have
\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}]=\frac{\gamma}{2+\gamma}d
and
\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{4}]=\frac{\gamma^{2}}{(2+\gamma)^{2}}(2d+d^{2})
. We also have
\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}^{\prime\top}]=\frac{\gamma}{2+\gamma}\mathbf{I}_{d}
. Thus

\displaystyle T\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\left[\alpha_{2}^{2}\big\|\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\big\|_{2}^{4}+\Big((\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu}+2\alpha_{2}(1-\alpha_{1})\mathbf{W})^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\Big)^{2}+\beta_{\mathbf{W}}^{2}-2\alpha_{2}\beta_{\mathbf{w}}\big\|\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\big\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\left[\alpha_{2}^{2}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma^2}{(2+\gamma)^2}$}}(2d+d^{2})+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{2+\gamma}$}}\left\|2\alpha_{2}(1-\alpha_{1})\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\beta_{\mathbf{W}}^{2}-2\alpha_{2}\beta_{\mathbf{W}}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{2+\gamma}$}}d\right]

\displaystyle\;\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\left[\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(1+\gamma)^2}{\gamma^2(2+\gamma)^2}$}}(2d+d^{2})+\frac{\gamma}{2+\gamma}\left\|\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2(1+\gamma)}{\gamma(2+\gamma)}$}}\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\beta_{\mathbf{W}}^{2}-2\beta_{\mathbf{W}}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1+\gamma}{\gamma(2+\gamma)}$}}d\right]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\left[\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(1+\gamma)^2}{\gamma^2(2+\gamma)^2}$}}(2d+d^{2})+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{2+\gamma}$}}\left\|\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2+4\gamma+\gamma^2}{\gamma(2+\gamma)}$}}\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\beta_{\mathbf{W}}^{2}-2\beta_{\mathbf{W}}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1+\gamma}{\gamma(2+\gamma)}$}}d\right]

\displaystyle\;\overset{(b)}{=}

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(1+\gamma)^2}{\gamma^2(2+\gamma)^2}$}}(2d+d^{2})+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(2+4\gamma+\gamma^2)^2}{\gamma(2+\gamma)^2(4+\gamma)}$}}d+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{2+\gamma}$}}\|\mu\|_{2}^{2}+\mathbb{E}\Big[\beta_{\mathbf{W}}^{2}-2\beta_{\mathbf{W}}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1+\gamma}{\gamma(2+\gamma)}$}}d\Big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(1+\gamma)^2}{\gamma^2(2+\gamma)^2}$}}d^{2}+\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 (1+\gamma)^2}{\gamma^2(2+\gamma)^2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(2+4\gamma+\gamma^2)^2}{\gamma(2+\gamma)^2(4+\gamma)}$}}\Big)d+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{2+\gamma}$}}\|\mu\|_{2}^{2}+\mathbb{E}\Big[\beta_{\mathbf{W}}^{2}-2\beta_{\mathbf{W}}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1+\gamma}{\gamma(2+\gamma)}$}}d\Big]\;.

In
(a)
, we have substituted in
\alpha_{1}=2/(2+\gamma)
and
\alpha_{2}=(\gamma+1)/\gamma^{2}
, and in
(b)
we have taken the expectation of the second term. Now re-express
\beta_{\mathbf{W}}
as

\displaystyle\mathbf{\beta}_{\mathbf{W}}\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2}{2 + \gamma}$}}\left\|\mathbf{W}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2}{2 + \gamma}$}}+1\right)\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{W}+\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma+1}{\gamma^2}$}}\left(1-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{2}\left\|\mathbf{W}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2}{2 + \gamma}$}}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma+1}{(2+\gamma)^2}$}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{W}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{4 + \gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{W}+\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma+ 3}{(2+\gamma)^2}$}}\left\|\mathbf{W}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{4 + \gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{W}+\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\;.

By noting that odd moments of
\mathbf{W}
vanish, we get that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[-2\beta_{\mathbf{W}}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1+\gamma}{\gamma(2+\gamma)}$}}d]\;=

\displaystyle\;-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2(1+\gamma)}{\gamma(2+\gamma)}$}}d\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma+ 3}{(2+\gamma)(4+\gamma)}$}}d+\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\Big)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2(1+\gamma)}{\gamma(2+\gamma)}$}}\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma+ 3}{(2+\gamma)(4+\gamma)}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{\gamma}$}}\Big)d^{2}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2(1+\gamma)}{\gamma(2+\gamma)}$}}d\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\;,

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\beta_{\mathbf{W}}^{2}]\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(\gamma+ 3)^2}{(2+\gamma)^2(4+\gamma)^2}$}}(2d+d^{2})+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{4+\gamma}{2+\gamma}$}}\|\mu\|_{2}^{2}+\Big(\|\mu\|_{2}^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\Big)^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2(\gamma+ 3)}{(2+\gamma)(4+\gamma)}$}}\Big(\|\mu\|_{2}^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\Big)d

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(\gamma+ 3)^2}{(2+\gamma)^2(4+\gamma)^2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{\gamma^2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2(\gamma+ 3)}{(2+\gamma)(4+\gamma)\gamma}$}}\Big)d^{2}+\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2(\gamma+ 3)^2}{(2+\gamma)^2(4+\gamma)^2}$}}\Big)d

\displaystyle\;+\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2}{\gamma}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2(\gamma+ 3)}{(2+\gamma)(4+\gamma)}$}}\Big)d\|\mu\|_{2}^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{4+\gamma}{2+\gamma}$}}\|\mu\|_{2}^{2}+\|\mu\|_{2}^{4}\;.

The coefficient of
d^{2}
in
T
can then be computed by noting
\gamma=\omega(1)
as

\displaystyle\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(1+\gamma)^2}{\gamma^2(2+\gamma)^2}$}}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2(1+\gamma)}{\gamma(2+\gamma)}$}}\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma+ 3}{(2+\gamma)(4+\gamma)}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{\gamma}$}}\Big)+\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(\gamma+ 3)^2}{(2+\gamma)^2(4+\gamma)^2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{\gamma^2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2(\gamma+ 3)}{(2+\gamma)(4+\gamma)\gamma}$}}\Big)

\displaystyle\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(2+\gamma)^2}{\gamma^2(4+\gamma)^2}$}}\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{\gamma^2}$}}+o\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{\gamma^2}$}}\Big)\;.

Similarly, the coefficient of
d
in
T
can be computed as

\displaystyle\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 (1+\gamma)^2}{\gamma^2(2+\gamma)^2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(2+4\gamma+\gamma^2)^2}{\gamma(2+\gamma)^2(4+\gamma)}$}}\Big)+\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2(\gamma+ 3)^2}{(2+\gamma)^2(4+\gamma)^2}$}}\Big)\;=

\displaystyle\;1+o(1)\;,

the coefficient of
d\|\mu\|_{2}^{2}
in
T
can be computed as

\displaystyle-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2(1+\gamma)}{\gamma(2+\gamma)}$}}+\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2}{\gamma}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2(\gamma+ 3)}{(2+\gamma)(4+\gamma)}$}}\Big)\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2(2+\gamma)}{\gamma(4+\gamma)}$}}\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2}{\gamma}$}}+o\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{\gamma}$}}\Big)\;,

the coefficient of
\|\mu\|_{2}^{2}
in
T
can be computed as

\displaystyle\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{2+\gamma}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{4+\gamma}{2+\gamma}$}}\;=\;2+o(1)\;,

and finally the coefficient of
\|\mu\|_{2}^{4}
in
T
is
1
. Combining the five computations of coefficients, we get that

\displaystyle T\;=\;4d+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d^2}{\gamma^2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2d\|\mu\|_2^2}{\gamma}$}}+2\|\mu\|_{2}^{2}+\|\mu\|_{2}^{4}+o\Big(d+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d^2}{\gamma^2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d\|\mu\|_2^2}{\gamma}$}}++\|\mu\|_{2}^{2}\Big)\;,

and therefore the desired quantity is given as

\displaystyle\sigma_{\rm full}^{2}\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}[u_{P}^{\rm KSD}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{X}^{\prime})^{2}]-D^{\rm KSD}(Q,P)^{2}

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{4 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}T-\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{2+\gamma}$}}\Big)^{d}\|\mu\|_{2}^{4}

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{4 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}T-\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{4 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma(4 + \gamma)}{(2 + \gamma)^2}$}}\right)^{d/2}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{4}

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{4 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\bigg(d+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d^2}{\gamma^2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2d \| \bmu\|_2^2}{\gamma}$}}+2\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}+\left(1-\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma(4 + \gamma)}{(2 + \gamma)^2}$}}\right)^{d/2}\right)\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{4}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\;+o\left(d+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d^2}{\gamma^2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d \| \bmu\|_2^2}{\gamma}$}}+\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\bigg)\;.

F.2.5
Proof for upper bound on
\mathbb{E}[|g^{\rm KSD}(\mathbf{X})|^{\nu}]

Fix
\nu>2
. We can apply Lemma
28
to rewrite the
\nu
-th moment of
g^{\rm KSD}(\mathbf{Z})
as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[|g^{\rm KSD}(\mathbf{X})|^{\nu}]\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{\nu d/2}\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\nu}{2(1 + \gamma)}$}}\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\left|\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 + \gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{X}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{1 + \gamma}$}}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\right|^{\nu}\right]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{\nu d/2}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(1 + \gamma) / \nu}{1 + (1 + \gamma) / \nu}$}}\right)^{d/2}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 + \gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{W}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{1 + \gamma}$}}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\right|^{\nu}\right]}_{\eqqcolon T}\;,

where
\mathbf{W}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{m},a^{2}I_{d}\right)
with
\mathbf{m}\coloneqq\frac{(1+\gamma)/\nu}{1+(1+\gamma)/\nu}\big(\mathbf{\mu}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\nu}{1 + \gamma}$}}\mathbf{\mu}\big)+\mathbf{\mu}
and
a^{2}\coloneqq\frac{(1+\gamma)/\nu}{1+(1+\gamma)/\nu}=\frac{1+\gamma}{1+\nu=\gamma}
. Defining
\mathbf{V}\coloneqq W-\mathbf{\mu}
so that
\mathbf{V}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,a^{2}I_{d})
, we have

\displaystyle T\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 + \gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}(\mathbf{V}+\mathbf{\mu})-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{1 + \gamma}$}}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\right|^{\nu}\right]\;=\;\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 + \gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{V}-\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\right|^{\nu}\right]

\displaystyle\;\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{\leq}}

\displaystyle\;2^{\nu-1}\left(\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 + \gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{\nu}\mathbb{E}[|\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{V}|^{\nu}]+\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2\nu}\right)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;2^{\nu-1}\left(C_{\nu}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 + \gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{\nu}a^{\nu}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{\nu}+\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2\nu}\right)\;,

where
(i)
follows by the fact that
|u+v|^{\nu}\leq 2^{\nu-1}(|u|^{\nu}+|v|^{\nu})
for any
u,v\in\mathbb{R}
, and in the last line we have computed the expectation by noting that
\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{V}
follows a univariate Gaussian distribution
\mathcal{N}(0,a^{2}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2})
and using its moment formula to yield
\mathbb{E}[|\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{V}|^{\nu}\leq C_{\nu}a^{\nu}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{\nu}
for some constant
C_{\nu}
that depends only on
\nu
.
Combining these and substituting the definition of
a^{2}
gives

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}[|g^{\rm KSD}(\mathbf{X})|^{\nu}]

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;2^{\nu-1}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{\nu d/2}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(1 + \gamma) / \nu}{1 + (1 + \gamma) / \nu}$}}\right)^{d/2}\left(C_{\nu}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 + \gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{\nu}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{1 + \nu+ \gamma}$}}\right)^{\nu/2}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{\nu}+\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2\nu}\right)

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{\nu d/2}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{1 + \nu+ \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\left(2^{3\nu/2-1}C_{\nu}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{\nu}+2^{\nu-1}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2\nu}\right)\;,

where in the last line we have used the assumption that
\nu>2
to yield the inequality

\displaystyle\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 + \gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{\nu}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{1 + \nu+ \gamma}$}}\right)^{\nu/2}\;=\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 + \gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{\nu}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 + \gamma}{1 + \nu+ \gamma}$}}\right)^{\nu/2}\;=\;2^{\nu/2}\times 1\;=\;2^{\nu/2}\;.

Defining the constants
C_{1}\coloneqq 2^{3\nu/2-1}C_{\nu}
and
C_{2}\coloneqq 2^{\nu-1}
completes the proof.

F.2.6
Proof for upper bound on
\mathbb{E}[|u_{P}^{\rm KSD}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{X}^{\prime})|^{\nu}]

Fix
\nu>2
. Define
\mathbf{Z}\coloneqq\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{\mu}
and
\mathbf{Z}^{\prime}\coloneqq\mathbf{X}^{\prime}-\mathbf{\mu}
so that
\mathbf{Z},\mathbf{Z}^{\prime}
are independent draws from
\mathcal{N}(0,I_{d})
. Using (
9
), we can write the
\nu
-th central moment as

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}[|u_{P}^{\rm KSD}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{X}^{\prime})|^{\nu}]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\left[\Big|\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2\gamma}$}}\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{X}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\Big(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\prime}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma+ 1}{\gamma^2}$}}\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{X}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\Big)\Big|^{\nu}\right]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\nu}{2\gamma}$}}\|\mathbf{Z}-\mathbf{Z}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\Big|(\mathbf{Z}+\mathbf{\mu})^{\top}(\mathbf{Z}^{\prime}+\mathbf{\mu})-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma+ 1}{\gamma^2}$}}\|\mathbf{Z}-\mathbf{Z}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\Big|^{\nu}\right]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma/ \nu}{2 + \gamma/ \nu}$}}\right)^{d/2}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\Big|(\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu})^{\top}(\mathbf{W}^{\prime}+(1-\alpha_{2})\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu})-\alpha_{1}\|\mathbf{W}-\mathbf{W}^{\prime}-(1-\alpha_{2})\mathbf{W}\|_{2}^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\Big|^{\nu}\right]}_{\eqqcolon T}\;,

(39)

where the last line follows by using Lemma
29
and defining the quantities

\alpha_{1}\coloneqq\frac{\gamma+1}{\gamma^{2}}
,
\alpha_{2}\coloneqq\frac{\gamma}{\nu+\gamma}
,
\alpha_{3}\coloneqq\frac{\gamma}{2\nu+\gamma}
, and

\displaystyle\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{0},\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma/ \nu}{1 + \gamma/ \nu}$}}I_{d}\right)=\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{0},\;\alpha_{2}I_{d}\right)\;,

\displaystyle\mathbf{W}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{0},\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma/ \nu}{2 + \gamma/ \nu}$}}I_{d}\right)=\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{0},\;\alpha_{3}I_{d}\right)\;,

while also noting that
1-\alpha_{2}=\frac{\gamma/\nu}{1+\gamma/\nu}\times\frac{\nu}{\gamma}=\frac{\nu}{\nu+\gamma}=1-\alpha_{2}
.
By a Jensen’s inequality, we get that

\displaystyle T\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\left[\Big|(\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu})^{\top}(\mathbf{W}^{\prime}+(1-\alpha_{2})\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu})-\alpha_{1}\left\|\alpha_{2}\mathbf{W}-\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\Big|^{\nu}\right]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\left[\Big|(\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu})^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}+(1-\alpha_{2})\|\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}+\alpha_{2}(\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu})^{\top}\mathbf{\mu}-\alpha_{1}\|\alpha_{2}\mathbf{W}-\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\Big|^{\nu}\right]

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;5^{\nu-1}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|(\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu})^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\big|^{\nu}+|1-\alpha_{2}|^{\nu}\|\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2\nu}+\alpha_{2}^{\nu}\big|(\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu})^{\top}\mathbf{\mu}\big|^{\nu}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\alpha_{1}^{\nu}\|\alpha_{2}\mathbf{W}-\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2\nu}+\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\Big)^{\nu}\Big]\;,

where the last line follows from a Jensen’s inequality applied to the convex function
x\mapsto|x|^{\nu}
.

We next seek to bound the expectation of each term individually. To bound
\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\big(\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu})^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\big|^{\nu}\big]
, we note that
(\mathbf{W}-\mathbf{\mu})^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}
conditioning on
\mathbf{W}
follows a normal distribution
\mathcal{N}(0,\alpha_{2}\|\mathbf{W}-\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2})
. Hence, using the moment formula of univariate Gaussians, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\big(\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu})^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\big|^{\nu}\big]\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\big[\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\big(\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu})^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\big|^{\nu}\big|\mathbf{W}\big]\big]\;=\;\mathbb{E}\big[C_{\nu}\alpha_{2}^{\nu}\|\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{\nu}\big]\;,

for some constant
C_{\nu}
constant depending only on
\nu
. By the convexity of the function
\mathbf{x}\mapsto\|\mathbf{x}\|_{2}^{\nu}
, we can bound the above term as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[C_{\nu}\alpha_{2}^{\nu}\|\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{\nu}\big]\;\leq

\displaystyle\;2^{\nu-1}C_{\nu}\alpha_{2}^{\nu}\big(\mathbb{E}\big[\|\mathbf{W}\|_{2}^{\nu}\big]+\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{\nu}\big)

\displaystyle\;\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{\leq}}

\displaystyle\;C_{\nu}\alpha_{2}^{\nu}\alpha_{3}^{\nu}\big(\mathbb{E}\big[\|\mathbf{W}\|_{2}^{2\nu}\big]\big)^{1/2}+C_{\nu}\alpha_{2}^{\nu}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{\nu}

\displaystyle\;\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(ii)}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\;C_{\nu}\alpha_{2}^{\nu}\alpha_{3}^{\nu}(d^{\nu/2}+o(d^{\nu/2}))+C_{\nu}\alpha_{2}^{\nu}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{\nu}

\displaystyle\;\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(iii)}}{{\leq}}

\displaystyle\;C_{\nu}d^{\nu/2}+o(d^{\nu/2})+C_{\nu}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{\nu}\;,

where
(i)
holds by a Jensen’s inequality, in
(ii)
we have noted that
\alpha_{3}^{-1}\|\mathbf{W}\|_{2}^{2}
follows a chi-squared distribution with
d
degrees of freedom and used the formula for its
\nu
-th moment, and
(iii)
follows since
\alpha_{2}=\frac{\gamma}{\nu+\gamma}<1
and
\alpha_{3}=\frac{\gamma}{2\nu+\gamma}<1
. The expectation of the second term can be bounded using a similar argument as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\|\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2\nu}\big]\;\leq\;2^{\nu-1}\big(\mathbb{E}\big[\|\mathbf{W}\|_{2}^{2\nu}\big]+\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2\nu}\big)\;=

\displaystyle\;2^{\nu-1}\big(\alpha_{3}^{\nu}d^{\nu}+o(d^{\nu})+\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2\nu}\big)

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;2^{\nu-1}d^{\nu}+o(d^{\nu})+2^{\nu-1}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2\nu}\;.

The expectation of the third term is

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\big|(\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\mu})^{\top}\mathbf{\mu}\big|^{\nu}\big]\;=\;\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\mathbf{W}^{\top}\mathbf{\mu}+\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\big|^{\nu}\big]\;\leq

\displaystyle\;2^{\nu-1}\big(\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\mathbf{W}^{\top}\mathbf{\mu}\big|^{\nu}\big]+\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2\nu}\big)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;2^{\nu-1}\big(\alpha_{3}^{\nu/2}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{\nu}+\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2\nu}\big)

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;2^{\nu-1}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{\nu}+2^{\nu-1}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2\nu}\;,

where the second last line holds as
\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{W}
is a univariate Gaussian with zero-mean and variance
\alpha_{3}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}
, and the last line holds again as
\alpha_{3}\leq 1
. It then remains to bound
\mathbb{E}\big[\|\alpha_{2}\mathbf{W}-\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2\nu}\big]
. Noting that
\alpha_{2}\mathbf{W}-\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\alpha_{2}(\alpha_{3}+1)I_{d})
, the random variable
\alpha_{2}^{-1}(\alpha_{3}+1)^{-1}\|\alpha_{2}\mathbf{W}-\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}
follows a chi-squared distribution with
d
degrees of freedom. A similar argument as before gives

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\|\alpha_{2}\mathbf{W}-\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2\nu}\big]\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\alpha_{2}^{\nu}(\alpha_{3}+1)^{\nu}\big(d^{\nu}+o(d^{\nu}))\;\leq\;2^{\nu}d^{\nu}+o(d^{\nu})\;,

where in the last inequality we have used the fact that
\alpha_{2}(\alpha_{3}+1)<2
. Combining these terms, we can bound
T
as

\displaystyle T\;\leq

\displaystyle\;5^{\nu-1}\Big[\big(C_{\nu}d^{\nu/2}+o(d^{\nu/2})+C_{\nu}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{\nu}\big)+|1-\alpha_{2}|^{\nu}2^{\nu-1}\big(d^{\nu}+o(d^{\nu})+\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2\nu}\big)

\displaystyle\;\qquad+2^{\nu-1}\big(\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{\nu}+\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2\nu}\big)+\alpha_{1}^{\nu}2^{\nu}\big(d^{\nu}+o(d^{\nu}))+\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\Big)^{\nu}\Big]\;.

To proceed, we note that
\alpha_{1}^{\nu}=\big(\frac{\gamma+1}{\gamma^{2}}\big)^{\nu}=\big(\frac{1}{\gamma}+\frac{1}{\gamma^{2}}\big)^{\nu}=\frac{1}{\gamma^{\nu}}+o\big(\frac{1}{\gamma^{\nu}}\big)
and that
(1-\alpha_{2})^{\nu}=\big(\frac{\nu}{\nu+\gamma})^{\nu}=\frac{1}{\gamma^{\nu}}+o\big(\frac{1}{\gamma^{\nu}}\big)
, since
\gamma=\omega(1)
by assumption. Therefore,

\displaystyle|1-\alpha_{2}|^{\nu}2^{\nu-1}\big(d^{\nu}+o(d^{\nu})+\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2\nu}\big)\;=\;2^{\nu}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d^\nu}{\gamma^\nu}$}}+2^{\nu}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bmu\|_2^{2\nu}}{\gamma^\nu}$}}+o\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d^\nu}{\gamma^\nu}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bmu\|_2^{2\nu}}{\gamma^\nu}$}}\Big)\;,

and

\displaystyle\alpha_{1}^{\nu}2^{\nu}\big(d^{\nu}+o(d^{\nu}))\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d^\nu}{\gamma^\nu}$}}+o\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d^\nu}{\gamma^\nu}$}}\Big)\;.

It then follows by grouping and rearranging that
T
can be bounded as

\displaystyle T\;\leq

\displaystyle\;5^{\nu-1}\Big[C_{\nu}d^{\nu/2}+o(d^{\nu/2})+C_{\nu}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{\nu}+2^{\nu}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d^\nu}{\gamma^\nu}$}}+2^{\nu}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bmu\|_2^{2\nu}}{\gamma^\nu}$}}+o\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d^\nu}{\gamma^\nu}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bmu\|_2^{2\nu}}{\gamma^\nu}$}}\Big)

\displaystyle\;\qquad+2^{\nu-1}\big(\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{\nu}+\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2\nu}\big)+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d^\nu}{\gamma^\nu}$}}+o\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d^\nu}{\gamma^\nu}$}}\Big)+\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\Big)^{\nu}\Big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;5^{\nu-1}\Big[C_{\nu}d^{\nu/2}+(2+2^{\nu})\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\Big)^{\nu}+(C_{\nu}+2^{\nu-1})\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{\nu}+2^{\nu-1}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2\nu}

\displaystyle\;\qquad+o\Big(d^{\nu/2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d^\nu}{\gamma^\nu}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bmu\|_2^{2\nu}}{\gamma^\nu}$}}\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;C_{3}d^{\nu/2}+C_{4}\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\Big)^{\nu}+C_{5}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{\nu}+C_{6}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2\nu}+o\Big(d^{\nu/2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d^\nu}{\gamma^\nu}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bmu\|_2^{2\nu}}{\gamma^\nu}$}}\Big)\;,

where in the last line we have redefined the constants:
C_{3}\coloneqq 5^{\nu-1}C_{\nu}
,
C_{4}\coloneqq 5^{\nu-1}(2+2^{\nu})
,
C_{5}\coloneqq 5^{\nu-1}(C_{\nu}+2^{\nu-1})
and
C_{6}\coloneqq 10^{\nu-1}
. The proof is finished by substituting this bound into (
39
) to yield

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[|u_{P}^{\rm KSD}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{X}^{\prime})|^{\nu}]\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{2\nu+ \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\Big(C_{3}d^{\nu/2}+C_{4}\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\Big)^{\nu}+C_{5}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{\nu}+C_{6}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2\nu}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+o\Big(d^{\nu/2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d^\nu}{\gamma^\nu}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bmu\|_2^{2\nu}}{\gamma^\nu}$}}\Big)\Big)\;.

F.2.7
Proof for verifying
1

First note that when
\gamma=\Omega(d)
, for any fixed
a,b,c>0
, we have that by a Taylor expansion,

\displaystyle\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{a+\gamma}{b+\gamma}$}}\Big)^{d/c}\;=\;\Big(1+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{a-b}{b+\gamma}$}}\Big)^{d/c}\;=

\displaystyle\;\exp\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{c}$}}\log\Big(1+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{a-b}{b+\gamma}$}}\Big)\Big)\;=\;\exp\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d(a-b)}{c(b+\gamma)}$}}+o\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma^2}$}}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\exp\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d(a-b)}{c(b+\gamma)}$}}\Big)\Big(1+o\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma^2}$}}\Big)\Big)\;=\;\Theta(1)\;.

Using this together with the assumption
\|\mu\|_{2}=\Theta(1)
and the moment bounds in Lemma
22
(iii)-(vi), we get that

\displaystyle\sigma_{\rm cond}^{2}\;=

\displaystyle\;\Theta\bigg(\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma^2}{(1 + \gamma)(3 + \gamma)}$}}\right)^{d/2}\bigg(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(2 + \gamma)^2}{(1 + \gamma)(3 + \gamma)}$}}+\left(1-\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(1 + \gamma)(3 + \gamma)}{(2 + \gamma)^2}$}}\right)^{d/2}\right)\bigg)\bigg)\;=\;\Theta(1)\;,

\displaystyle\sigma_{\rm full}^{2}\;=

\displaystyle\;\Theta\bigg(\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{4 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\bigg(d+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d^2}{\gamma^2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2d}{\gamma}$}}+\left(1-\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma(4 + \gamma)}{(2 + \gamma)^2}$}}\right)^{d/2}\right)\;+o\left(d+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d^2}{\gamma^2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\right)\bigg)\bigg)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\Theta\Big(d+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d^2}{\gamma^2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2d}{\gamma}$}}\Big)\;=\;\Theta\Big(d+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d^2}{\gamma^2}$}}\Big)\;,

and for
\nu\in(2,3]
,

\displaystyle M_{\rm cond;\nu}^{\nu}\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}[|g^{\rm KSD}(\mathbf{X})|^{\nu}]\;=\;O\Big(\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{\nu d/2}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{1 + \nu+ \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\Big)\;=\;O(1)\;,
