\displaystyle M_{\rm full;\nu}^{\nu}\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}[|u_{P}^{\rm KSD}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{X}^{\prime})|^{\nu}]\;=\;O\Big(\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{2\nu+ \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\Big(d^{\nu/2}+\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\Big)^{\nu}\Big)\Big)\;=\;O\Big(d^{\nu/2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d^\nu}{\gamma^\nu}$}}\Big)\;.

This implies that

\displaystyle\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\Mcondnu}{\sigcond}$}}\;

\displaystyle=\;O(1)\;,

and

\displaystyle\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\Mfullnu}{\sigfull}$}}\;

\displaystyle=\;O\bigg(\Big(d^{1/2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\Big)^{-1}\Big(d^{1/2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{d}{\gamma}$}}\Big)\bigg)\;=\;O(1)\;.

In other words,
\frac{M_{\rm cond;\nu}}{\sigma_{\rm cond}}
and
\frac{M_{\rm full;\nu}}{\sigma_{\rm full}}
are both bounded by finite,
d
-independent constants, which verifies
1
.

F.3
Proof for Lemma
24

F.3.1
Proof for
g^{\rm mmd}(\mathbf{z})

Recall that the MMD U-statistic is
u^{\rm MMD}(\mathbf{z},\mathbf{z}^{\prime})=\kappa(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}^{\prime})+\kappa(\mathbf{y},\mathbf{y}^{\prime})-\kappa(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}^{\prime})-\kappa(\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{y})
for
\mathbf{z}\coloneqq(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})
and
\mathbf{z}^{\prime}=(\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{y}^{\prime})
. Taking expectation with respect to the second argument, we have

\displaystyle g^{\rm mmd}(\mathbf{z})\;\coloneqq\;\mathbb{E}[u^{\rm MMD}(z,Z^{\prime})]\;=\;\mathbb{E}[\kappa(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{X}^{\prime})+\kappa(\mathbf{y},\mathbf{Y}^{\prime})-\kappa(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{Y}^{\prime})-\kappa(\mathbf{X}^{\prime},\mathbf{y})]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bx- \bX'\|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}\Big)+\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\|\by- \bY'\|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}\Big)-\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bx- \bY'\|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}\Big)-\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX' - \by\|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}\Big)\Big]\;.

We can apply Lemma
28
to compute each term. For example, setting
\mathbf{a}_{1}=1,\mathbf{a}_{2}=\gamma,\mathbf{m}_{1}=\mathbf{\mu}
and
\mathbf{m}_{2}=\mathbf{x}
in Lemma
28
, the first term simplifies to

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bx- \bX'\|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}\right)\right]\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2(1 + \gamma)}$}}\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\;.

Computing similarly the other terms yields the desired result:

\displaystyle g^{\rm mmd}(\mathbf{z})\;

\displaystyle=\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\Big[e^{-\frac{1}{2(1+\gamma)}\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}}+e^{-\frac{1}{2(1+\gamma)}\|\mathbf{y}\|_{2}^{2}}-e^{-\frac{1}{2(1+\gamma)}\|\mathbf{x}\|_{2}^{2}}-e^{-\frac{1}{2(1+\gamma)}\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}}\Big]\;.

F.3.2
Proof for
D^{\rm MMD}(Q,P)

This is a special case of
Ramdas et al. 2015
with
\mathbf{\mu}_{1}=0
,
\mathbf{\mu}_{2}=\mathbf{\mu}
and
\Sigma=I_{d}
. Alternatively, applying Lemma
28
to compute each term in
\mathbb{E}[g(Z)]
yields the same result.

F.3.3
Proof for
\sigma_{\rm cond}^{2}

For
\mathbf{Z}=(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y})
, the second moment of
g(\mathbf{Z})
is

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[g(\mathbf{Z})^{2}]\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\bigg(e^{-\frac{1}{2(1+\gamma)}\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}}+e^{-\frac{1}{2(1+\gamma)}\|\mathbf{y}\|_{2}^{2}}-e^{-\frac{1}{2(1+\gamma)}\|\mathbf{x}\|_{2}^{2}}-e^{-\frac{1}{2(1+\gamma)}\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}}\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{ \| \bX- \bmu\|_2^2}{1 + \gamma}$}}\Big)+\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bY\|_2^2}{1 + \gamma}$}}\Big)+\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX\|_2^2}{1 + \gamma}$}}\Big)

\displaystyle\;\qquad\qquad\quad+\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bY- \bmu\|_2^2}{1 + \gamma}$}}\Big)+2\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX- \bmu\|_2^2}{2(1 + \gamma)}$}}\Big)\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bY\|_2^2 }{2(1 + \gamma)}$}}\Big)

\displaystyle\;\qquad\qquad\quad-2\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{ \| \bX- \bmu\|_2^2 + \| \bX\|_2^2 }{2(1 + \gamma)}$}}\Big)-2\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX- \bmu\|_2^2}{2(1 + \gamma)}$}}\Big)\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bY- \bmu\|_2^2 }{2(1 + \gamma)}$}}\Big)

\displaystyle\;\qquad\qquad\quad-2\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX\|_2^2 }{2(1 + \gamma)}$}}\Big)\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bY\|_2^2 }{2(1 + \gamma)}$}}\Big)-2\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bY\|_2^2 + \| \bY- \bmu\|_2^2 }{2(1 + \gamma)}$}}\Big)

\displaystyle\;\qquad\qquad\quad+2\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2(1 + \gamma)}$}}\|\mathbf{X}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2(1 + \gamma)}$}}\|\mathbf{Y}-\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\bigg]\;.

We can compute each term by applying Lemma
28
. Noting that
\mathbf{Y}-\mathbf{\mu}
and
\mathbf{X}
are equal in distribution, and also that Lemma
28
depends on
\mathbf{m}_{1}
and
\mathbf{m}_{2}
only through their difference, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX- \bmu\|_2^2}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)\right]\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{ \| \bY\|_2^2}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)\right]\;=\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{3 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\;,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX\|_2^2 }{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)\right]\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bY- \bmu\|_2^2 }{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)\right]\;=\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{3 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{ \| \bmu\|_2^2}{3 + \gamma}$}}\right)\;,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX- \bmu\|_2^2}{2(1 + \gamma)}$}}\right)\right]\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bY\|_2^2}{2(1 + \gamma)}$}}\right)\right]\;=\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\;,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX\|_2^2}{2(1 + \gamma)}$}}\right)\right]\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bY- \bmu\|_2^2}{2(1 + \gamma)}$}}\right)\right]\;=\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \mu\|_2^2}{2 (2 + \gamma)}$}}\right)\;.

It remains to calculate the expectations of the sixth and ninth terms, which involve two differently centred quadratic forms of
\mathbf{X}
and
\mathbf{Y}
respectively. The sixth term simplifies to

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX- \bmu\|_2^2 + \| \bX\|_2^2}{2(1 + \gamma)}$}}\right)\right]\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2(1 + \gamma)}$}}\left(2\|\mathbf{X}\|_{2}^{2}-2\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{X}+\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{1 + \gamma}$}}\left\|\mathbf{X}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\bmu}{2}$}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)\right]\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{ \| \bmu\|_2^2}{4(1 + \gamma)}$}}\right)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{3 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bmu\|_2^2}{4(3 + \gamma)}$}}\right)\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bmu\|_2^2}{4(1 + \gamma)}$}}\right)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{3 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 + \gamma}{(2(3 + \gamma)(1 + \gamma)}$}}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\;,

(40)

and a similar calculation gives,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bY\|_2^2 + \| \bY- \bmu\|_2^2}{2(1 + \gamma)}$}}\right)\right]\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{3 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 + \gamma}{(2(3 + \gamma)(1 + \gamma)}$}}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\;.

Combining the above identities yields

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[g(Z)^{2}]\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d}\bigg(2\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{3 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}+2\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{3 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{ \| \bmu\|_2^2}{3 + \gamma}$}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad+2\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d}-4\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{3 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 + \gamma}{2(3 + \gamma)(1 + \gamma)}$}}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad-4\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \mu\|_2^2}{2 (2 + \gamma)}$}}\right)+2\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bmu\|_2^2}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)\bigg)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;2\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{3 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}

\displaystyle\;\quad\times\bigg(1+\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{ \| \bmu\|_2^2}{3 + \gamma}$}}\right)+\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{3 + \gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}-2\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(2 + \gamma)\| \bmu\|_2^2}{2(3 + \gamma)(1 + \gamma)}$}}\right)

\displaystyle\;\qquad\;\;\;-2\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \mu\|_2^2}{2 (2 + \gamma)}$}}\right)+\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bmu\|_2^2}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)\bigg)\;.

By noting that
D^{\rm MMD}(Q,P)^{2}=4\big(\frac{\gamma}{2+\gamma}\big)^{d}\Big(1-\exp\big(-\frac{\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}}{2(2+\gamma)}\big)\Big)^{2}
, we hence obtain

\displaystyle\sigma_{\rm cond}^{2}\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}[g(Z)^{2}]-D^{\rm MMD}(Q,P)^{2}\;=\;\mathbb{E}[g(Z)^{2}]-4\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d}\left[1-\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bmu\|_2^2 }{2 (2 + \gamma) }$}}\right)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;2\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{3 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}

\displaystyle\;\times\bigg(1+\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bmu\|_2^2 }{3 + \gamma}$}}\right)+2\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{3 + \gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bmu\|_2^2 }{2(2 + \gamma)}$}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad-2\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{7 + 5\gamma}{4 (1 + \gamma)(3 + \gamma)}$}}\|\mu\|_{2}^{2}\right)-\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{3 + \gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}

\displaystyle\qquad-\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{3 + \gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bmu\|_2^2 }{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)\bigg)\;,

as required.

F.3.4
Proof for
\sigma_{\rm full}^{2}

The second moment is

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}[u^{\rm MMD}(Z,Z^{\prime})^{2}]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\bigg[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX- \bX'\|_2^2}{\gamma}$}}\right)+\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bY- \bY' \|_2^2}{\gamma}$}}\right)+\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX- \bY' \|_2^2}{\gamma}$}}\right)+\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX' - \bY\|_2^2}{\gamma}$}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\;+2\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX- \bX' \|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bY- \bY' \|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}\right)-2\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX- \bX' \|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX- \bY' \|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\;-2\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX- \bX' \|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX' - \bY\|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}\right)-2\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bY- \bY' \|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX- \bY' \|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\;-2\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bY- \bY' \|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX' - \bY\|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}\right)+2\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX- \bY' \|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX' - \bY\|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}\right)\bigg]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\bigg[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX- \bX'\|_2^2}{\gamma}$}}\right)+\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bY- \bY' \|_2^2}{\gamma}$}}\right)\bigg]+2\mathbb{E}\bigg[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX- \bY' \|_2^2}{\gamma}$}}\right)\bigg]

\displaystyle\;+2\mathbb{E}\bigg[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX- \bX' \|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bY- \bY' \|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}\right)\bigg]-4\mathbb{E}\bigg[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX- \bX' \|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX- \bY' \|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}\right)\bigg]

\displaystyle\;-4\mathbb{E}\bigg[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bY- \bY' \|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX- \bY' \|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}\right)\bigg]+2\left(\mathbb{E}\bigg[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX- \bY' \|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}\right)\bigg]\right)^{2}\;,

where the second equality follows by the fact that
X,X^{\prime}
and
Y,Y^{\prime}
are respectively independent copies from
Q
and
P
. To calculate each term, we apply Lemma
29
to yield

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX- \bX'\|_2^2}{\gamma}$}}\right)\bigg]\;=\;\mathbb{E}\bigg[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bY- \bY' \|_2^2}{\gamma}$}}\right)\bigg]\;=\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma/2}{2 + \gamma/ 2}$}}\right)^{d/2}\;=\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{4 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\;,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX- \bX'\|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}\right)\bigg]\;=\;\mathbb{E}\bigg[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bY- \bY' \|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}\right)\bigg]\;=\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\;,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[e^{-\frac{\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}}{\gamma}}\bigg]\;=\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{4 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bmu\|_2^2}{4 + \gamma}$}}\right)\;,\;\;\mathbb{E}\bigg[e^{-\frac{\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}}{2\gamma}}\bigg]\;=\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bmu\|_2^2}{2(2 + \gamma)}$}}\right)\;.

Let
T_{1}\coloneqq\mathbb{E}\Big[\exp\Big(-\frac{\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{X}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}}{2\gamma}-\frac{\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}}{2\gamma}\Big)\Big]
and
T_{2}\coloneqq\mathbb{E}\Big[\exp\Big(-\frac{\|\mathbf{Y}-\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}}{2\gamma}-\frac{\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}}{2\gamma}\Big)\Big]
, which are the only remaining terms to compute. The first term can be simplified as

\displaystyle T_{1}\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbb{E}\bigg[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX- \bX' \|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}\right)\Big|\mathbf{X}\bigg]\mathbb{E}\bigg[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX- \bY' \|_2^2}{2\gamma}$}}\right)\Big|\mathbf{X}\bigg]\bigg]

\displaystyle\;\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX\|_2^2}{2 (1 + \gamma)}$}}\right)\times\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX- \bmu\|_2^2}{2 (1 + \gamma)}$}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d}\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX\|_2^2}{2 (1 + \gamma)}$}}\right)\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bX- \bmu\|_2^2}{2 (1 + \gamma)}$}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\;\overset{(b)}{=}

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 + \gamma}{3 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 + \gamma}{2(3 + \gamma)(1 + \gamma)}$}}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{3 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 + \gamma}{2(3 + \gamma)(1 + \gamma)}$}}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\;,

where in
(a)
we have applied Lemma
28
to compute the conditional expectations, and
(b)
follows from substituting (
40
). The second term can be simplified with a similar calculation as,

\displaystyle T_{2}\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{3 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 + \gamma}{2(3 + \gamma)(1 + \gamma)}$}}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\;.

Collecting all terms gives

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[u^{\rm MMD}(Z,Z^{\prime})^{2}]\;=

\displaystyle\;2\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{4 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}+2\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{4 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bmu\|_2^2}{4 + \gamma}$}}\right)+2\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d}

\displaystyle\;-8\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{3 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d/2}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 + \gamma}{2(3 + \gamma)(1 + \gamma)}$}}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\;+2\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)^{d}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \mu\|_2^2}{2 + \gamma}$}}\right)\;.

By noting that

\displaystyle D^{\rm MMD}(Q,P)^{2}\;=

\displaystyle\;4\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\Big)^{d}\Big(1-\exp\big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bmu\|_2^2 }{2 (2 + \gamma) }$}}\big)\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;4\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\Big)^{d}\Big(1-\exp\big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bmu\|_2^2 }{2 + \gamma}$}}\big)+2\exp\big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bmu\|_2^2 }{2 (2 + \gamma) }$}}\big)\Big)\;,

the variance takes the following form after subtracting
D^{\rm MMD}(Q,P)^{2}
and collecting similar terms

\displaystyle\sigma_{\rm full}^{2}\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}[u^{\rm MMD}(Z,Z^{\prime})^{2}]-D^{\rm MMD}(Q,P)^{2}

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;2\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{4 + \gamma}$}}\Big)^{d/2}\Big(1+\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bmu\|_2^2}{4 + \gamma}$}}\Big)\Big)-2\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\Big)^{d}

\displaystyle\;-8\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{1 + \gamma}$}}\Big)^{d/2}\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{3 + \gamma}$}}\Big)^{d/2}\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 + \gamma}{2(3 + \gamma)(1 + \gamma)}$}}\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\;-2\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\Big)^{d}\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \mu\|_2^2}{2 + \gamma}$}}\Big)+8\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{2 + \gamma}$}}\Big)^{d}\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bmu\|_2^2}{2(2 + \gamma)}$}}\Big)\;,

which completes the proof.

F.4
Proof of Lemma
25

With a linear kernel and under the stated assumption, the MMD statistic is

\displaystyle u^{\rm MMD}(\mathbf{z},\mathbf{z}^{\prime})\;=\;\mathbf{x}^{\top}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}+\mathbf{y}^{\top}\mathbf{y}^{\prime}-\mathbf{x}^{\top}\mathbf{y}^{\prime}-\mathbf{y}^{\top}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\;,\quad\text{ where }\;\;\mathbf{z}=(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}),\mathbf{z}^{\prime}=(\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{y}^{\prime})\in\mathbb{R}^{2d}\;.

F.4.1
Proof for
g^{\rm mmd}(\mathbf{z})
and
D^{\rm MMD}(Q,P)

The expression for
g^{\rm mmd}
can be computed as

\displaystyle g^{\rm mmd}(\mathbf{z})\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}[u^{\rm MMD}(\mathbf{z},\mathbf{Z}^{\prime})]\;=\;\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{x}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\prime}+\mathbf{y}^{\top}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}-\mathbf{x}^{\top}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}-\mathbf{y}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\prime}]\;=\;\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{y}\;.

The formula for
D^{\rm MMD}(Q,P)
then follows as

\displaystyle D^{\rm MMD}(Q,P)\;=\;\mathbb{E}[u^{\rm MMD}(\mathbf{Z},\mathbf{Z}^{\prime})]\;=\;\mathbb{E}[g^{\rm mmd}(\mathbf{Z})]\;=\;\mathbb{E}[\mu^{\top}\mathbf{X}-\mu^{\top}\mathbf{Y}]\;=\;\mu^{\top}\mu\;=\;\|\mu\|_{2}^{2}\;.

F.4.2
Proof for
\sigma_{\rm cond}^{2}

A direct computation gives

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[g^{\rm mmd}(\mathbf{Z})^{2}]\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\Big[(\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{X})^{2}+(\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{Y})^{2}-2\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{X}\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{Y}\Big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}(\Sigma+\mathbf{\mu}\mathbf{\mu}^{\top})\mathbf{\mu}+\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\Sigma\mathbf{\mu}\;=\;2\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\Sigma\mathbf{\mu}+\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{4}\;.

Therefore,
\sigma_{\rm cond}^{2}=\mathbb{E}[g^{\rm mmd}(\mathbf{Z})^{2}]-D^{\rm MMD}(Q,P)^{2}=2\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\Sigma\mathbf{\mu}
, as required.

F.4.3
Proof for
\sigma_{\rm full}^{2}

The second moment is

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[u^{\rm MMD}(\mathbf{Z},\mathbf{Z}^{\prime})^{2}]\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\big[(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\prime})^{2}+(\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime})^{2}+(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime})^{2}+(\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\prime})^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\;\;+2\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}-2\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}-2\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad\;\;-2\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}-2\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{Y}\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\prime}+2\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\prime}\big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\big[(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\prime})^{2}+(\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime})^{2}+(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime})^{2}+(\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\prime})^{2}\big]\;.

In the last equality, we have noted that the cross-terms vanish since
\mathbf{X},\mathbf{X}^{\prime},\mathbf{Y}
and
\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}
are mutually independent and
\mathbf{X},\mathbf{X}^{\prime}
are zero-mean. A direct computation gives

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\prime})^{2}\big]\;=

\displaystyle\;\text{\rm Tr}\big(\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{X}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\prime}(\mathbf{X}^{\prime})^{\top}]\big)\;=\;\text{\rm Tr}(\Sigma^{2})\;,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[(\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime})^{2}\big]\;=

\displaystyle\;\text{\rm Tr}\big(\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{Y}\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}(\mathbf{Y}^{\prime})^{\top}]\big)\;=\;\text{\rm Tr}\big((\Sigma+\mathbf{\mu}\mathbf{\mu}^{\top})^{2}\big)\;=\;\text{\rm Tr}(\Sigma^{2})+2\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\Sigma\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}+\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{4}\;,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[(\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{Y}^{\prime})^{2}\big]\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\big[(\mathbf{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\prime})^{2}\big]\;=\;\text{\rm Tr}(\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{Y}(\mathbf{Y}^{\prime})^{\top}\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{X}^{\prime})^{\top}])\;=\;\text{\rm Tr}(\Sigma^{2})+\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\Sigma\mathbf{\mu}\;.

Therefore,
\mathbb{E}[u^{\rm MMD}(\mathbf{Z},\mathbf{Z}^{\prime})^{2}]=4\text{\rm Tr}(\Sigma^{2})+4\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\Sigma\mathbf{\mu}+\|\mathbf{\mu}\|_{2}^{4}
, and

\displaystyle\sigma_{\rm full}^{2}\;=\;\mathbb{E}[u^{\rm MMD}(\mathbf{Z},\mathbf{Z}^{\prime})^{2}]-D^{\rm MMD}(Q,P)^{2}\;=\;4\text{\rm Tr}(\Sigma^{2})+4\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\Sigma\mathbf{\mu}\;,

which completes the proof.

F.4.4
Proof for upper bound on
M_{\rm cond;3}^{3}

The 3rd absolute centred moment of
g^{\rm mmd}(\mathbf{Z})
satisfies

\displaystyle M_{\rm cond;3}^{3}=\mathbb{E}[|g^{\rm mmd}(\mathbf{Z})-\mathbb{E}[g^{\rm mmd}(\mathbf{Z})]|^{3}]=\mathbb{E}[|\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{Y}-\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{\mu}|^{3}]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[|\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{Y}-\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{V}|^{3}]\;,

where we have defined
\mathbf{V}\coloneqq\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{\mu}
so that
\mathbf{V}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\Sigma)
. Noting that
|a+b|^{3}\leq 2^{3-1}(|a|^{3}+|b|^{3})
for any
a,b\in\mathbb{R}
by Jensen’s inequality, we can bound the above as

\displaystyle M_{\rm cond;3}^{3}=\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{Y}-\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{V}\big|^{m}\big]\leq 4\mathbb{E}[|\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{Y}|^{3}+|\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\mathbf{V}|^{3}]\overset{(a)}{=}8C^{\prime}(\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\Sigma\mathbf{\mu})^{3/2}\overset{(b)}{=}C(\mathbf{\mu}^{\top}\Sigma\mathbf{\mu})^{3/2}\;.

In
(a)
we have noted that the absolute 3rd moment of a univariate normal variable
\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^{2})
is given as
C^{\prime}\sigma^{3}
for some absolute constant
C^{\prime}
. In
(b)
, we have defined
C\coloneqq 8C^{\prime}
.

F.4.5
Proof for upper bound on
M_{\rm full;3}^{3}

For any
\mathbf{z}=(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}),\mathbf{z}^{\prime}=(\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{z}^{\prime})\in\mathbb{R}^{2d}
we have

\displaystyle u^{\rm MMD}(\mathbf{z},\mathbf{z}^{\prime})\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbf{x}^{\top}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}+\mathbf{y}^{\top}\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{x}^{\top}\mathbf{y}^{\prime}-\mathbf{y}^{\top}\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\;=\;(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{y})^{\top}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}-\mathbf{y}^{\prime})\;.

Write
\mathbf{V}\coloneqq\mathbf{X}-\mu
and
\mathbf{V}^{\prime}\coloneqq\mathbf{X}-\mu
so that
\mathbf{V},\mathbf{V}^{\prime}\overset{i.i.d.}{\sim}\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{0},\Sigma)
. We can compute the 3rd absolute central moment as

\displaystyle M_{\rm full;3}^{3}\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}[|u^{\rm MMD}(\mathbf{Z},\mathbf{Z}^{\prime})-\mathbb{E}[u^{\rm MMD}(\mathbf{Z},\mathbf{Z}^{\prime})]|^{3}]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}[|(\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{Y})^{\top}(\mathbf{X}^{\prime}-\mathbf{Y}^{\prime})-\mu^{\top}\mu|^{3}]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}[|(\mathbf{V}+\mu-\mathbf{Y})^{\top}(\mathbf{V}^{\prime}+\mu-\mathbf{Y}^{\prime})-\mu^{\top}\mu|^{3}]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}[|(\mathbf{V}-\mathbf{Y})^{\top}(\mathbf{V}^{\prime}-\mathbf{Y}^{\prime})+\mu^{\top}(\mathbf{V}^{\prime}-\mathbf{Y}^{\prime})+(\mathbf{V}-\mathbf{Y})^{\top}\mu|^{3}]\;.

By a Jensen’s inequality applied to the convex function
x\mapsto|x|^{3}
and a Hölder’s inequality, we get that

\displaystyle M_{\rm full;3}^{3}\;\leq

\displaystyle\;9\big(\mathbb{E}\big[|(\mathbf{V}-\mathbf{Y})^{\top}(\mathbf{V}^{\prime}-\mathbf{Y}^{\prime})|^{3}\big]+\mathbb{E}\big[|\mu^{\top}(\mathbf{V}^{\prime}-\mathbf{Y}^{\prime})|^{3}\big]+\mathbb{E}\big[|(\mathbf{V}-\mathbf{Y})^{\top}\mu|^{3}\big]\big)

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;9\big(\mathbb{E}\big[|\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{U}^{\prime}|^{3}\big]+2\mathbb{E}\big[|\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mu|^{3}\big]\big)\;.

In the last line, we have used that
\mathbf{U}\coloneqq\mathbf{V}-\mathbf{Y}
and
\mathbf{U}^{\prime}\coloneqq\mathbf{V}^{\prime}-\mathbf{Y}^{\prime}
are identically distributed. In fact they are both
\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{0},2\Sigma)
. The second expectation can be computed by the formula for the absolute 3rd moment of a univariate Gaussian as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[|\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mu|^{3}\big]\;=\;C^{\prime}(\mu^{\top}\Sigma\mu)^{3/2}\;

where
C^{\prime}
is some absolute constant. Similarly the first expectation can be computed first by noting that
\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{U}^{\prime}
conditioning on
\mathbf{U}
is a univariate Gaussian and secondly by using the moment formula for a Gaussian quadratic form Lemma
27
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[|\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{U}^{\prime}|^{3}

\displaystyle\big]\;=\;\mathbb{E}\big[\mathbb{E}[|\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{U}^{\prime}|^{3}|\mathbf{U}]\big]\;=\;C^{\prime}\mathbb{E}\big[(\mathbf{U}^{\top}\Sigma\mathbf{U})^{3/2}\big]

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;C^{\prime}\mathbb{E}\big[(\mathbf{U}^{\top}\Sigma\mathbf{U})^{3}\big]^{1/2}=C^{\prime}\big(\text{\rm Tr}(\Sigma^{2})^{3}+6\text{\rm Tr}(\Sigma^{2})\text{\rm Tr}(\Sigma^{4})+8\text{\rm Tr}(\Sigma^{6})\big)\leq 15C^{\prime}\text{\rm Tr}(\Sigma^{2})^{3}\;.

In the last line, we have noted that
\text{\rm Tr}(A^{m})\leq\text{\rm Tr}(A)^{m}
for
m\in\mathbb{N}
and positive semi-definite matrix
A
, which holds by expressing each trace as a sum of eigenvalues and applying the Hölder’s inequality. Combining the two computations and redefining constants, we get that for some constant
C
,

\displaystyle M_{\rm full;3}^{3}\;\leq

\displaystyle\;C\big(\text{\rm Tr}(\Sigma^{2})^{3}+(\mu^{\top}\Sigma\mu)^{3/2}\big)\;\leq\;C\big(\text{\rm Tr}(\Sigma^{2})+\mu^{\top}\Sigma\mu\big)^{3/2}\;.

F.4.6
Proof for verifying
1

By the bounds from
(iii)
-
(vi)
, there exists absolute constants
C_{1},C_{2}
such that

\displaystyle\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\Mcondthree}{\sigcond}$}}\;\leq\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{ C_1^{1/3} (\mu^\top\Sigma\mu)^{1/2}}{2^{1/2} (\bmu^\top\Sigma\bmu)^{1/2}}$}}\;=\;2^{-1/2}C_{1}^{1/3}\;,

\displaystyle\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\Mfullthree}{\sigfull}$}}\;\leq\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{ C_2^{1/3} \big(\Tr(\Sigma^2) + \mu^\top\Sigma\mu\big)^{1/2}}{2 \big(\Tr(\Sigma^2) + \mu^\top\Sigma\mu\big)^{1/2}}$}}\;=\;2^{-1}C_{2}^{1/3}\;,

which prove that
1
holds with
\nu=3
.

Appendix G
Proofs for
Appendix

B

G.1
Proofs for
Section

B.1

The proof of Lemma
26
combines the following two results:

Lemma 44

(Theorem 2,
von Bahr and Esseen 1965
)
.

Fix
\nu\in[1,2]
. For a martingale difference sequence
Y_{1},\ldots,Y_{n}
taking values in
\mathbb{R}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\msum_{i=1}^{n}Y_{i}\big|^{\nu}\big]\;\leq\;2\msum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[|Y_{i}|^{\nu}]\;.

Lemma 45

(
Dharmadhikari et al. 1968
)
.

Fix
\nu\geq 2
. For a martingale difference sequence
Y_{1},\ldots,Y_{n}
taking values in
\mathbb{R}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\msum_{i=1}^{n}Y_{i}\big|^{\nu}\big]\;\leq\;C_{\nu}n^{\nu/2-1}\msum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[|Y_{i}|^{\nu}]\;,

where
C_{\nu}=(8(\nu-1)\max\{1,2^{\nu-3}\})^{\nu}
.

Proof of Lemma
26

We first consider the upper bound. For
\nu\in[1,2]
, the result follows directly from the Von Bahn-Esseen inequality as stated below in Lemma
44
, and for
\nu>1
, the result follows directly from Lemma
45
. As for the lower bound, by Theorem 9 of
Burkholder 1966
, there exists an absolute constant
c_{\nu}>0
depending only on
\nu
such that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\msum_{i=1}^{n}Y_{i}\big|^{\nu}\big]\;\geq\;c_{\nu}\,\mathbb{E}\big[\big(\msum_{i=1}^{n}Y_{i}^{2}\big)^{\nu/2}\big]\;.

For
\nu\in[1,2]
, by applying Jensen’s inequality on the concave function
x\mapsto x^{\nu/2}
, we get that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\msum_{i=1}^{n}Y_{i}\big|^{\nu}\big]\;\geq

\displaystyle\;c_{\nu}\,\mathbb{E}\big[\big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n}$}}\msum_{i=1}^{n}nY_{i}^{2}\big)^{\nu/2}\big]\;\geq\;c_{\nu}\,n^{\nu/2-1}\msum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[|Y_{i}|^{\nu}]\;.

For
\nu>2
, by noting that
(a+b)^{\nu/2}\geq a^{\nu/2}+b^{\nu/2}
for
a,b\geq 0
, we get that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\msum_{i=1}^{n}Y_{i}\big|^{\nu}\big]\;\geq

\displaystyle\;c_{\nu}\,\mathbb{E}\big[\msum_{i=1}^{n}\big(Y_{i}^{2}\big)^{\nu/2}\big]\;\geq\;c_{\nu}\msum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[|Y_{i}|^{\nu}]\;.

Combining the two results above give the desired bound.

\jmlrQED

Proof of Lemma
28

A direct computation gives

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\left[f(\mathbf{X})\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2a_2^2}$}}\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{m}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{(2\pi)^{d/2} a_1^{d}}$}}\int f(\mathbf{x})\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2a_2^2}$}}\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{m}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2a_1^2}$}}\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{m}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\right)d\mathbf{x}

\displaystyle=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{(2\pi)^{d/2} a_1^{d}}$}}\int f(\mathbf{x})\exp\bigg(\underbrace{-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2}$}}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\|\bx\|_2^2}{a_2^2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\|\bm_2\|_2^2}{a_2^2}$}}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2\bm_2^\top\bx}{a_2^2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\|\bx\|_2^2}{a_1^2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\|\bm_1\|_2^2}{a_1^2}$}}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2\bm_1^\top\bx}{a_1^2}$}}\right)}_{=:T}\bigg)d\mathbf{x}\;.

Simplifying
T
by completing the square yields

\displaystyle T\;=

\displaystyle\;-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2}$}}\bigg(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\|\bx\|_2^2}{a_2^2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\|\bx\|_2^2}{a_1^2}$}}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2\bm_2^\top\bx}{a_2^2}$}}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2\bm_1^\top\bx}{a_1^2}$}}\bigg)-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2}$}}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\|\bm_2\|_2^2}{a_2^2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\|\bm_1\|_2^2}{a_1^2}$}}\right)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{a_1^2 + a_2^2}{2 a_1^2 a_2^2}$}}\Bigg(\|\mathbf{x}\|_{2}^{2}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2a_1^2 a_2^2}{a_1^2 + a_2^2}$}}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\bm_2}{a_2^2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\bm_1}{a_1^2}$}}\right)^{\top}\mathbf{x}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{a_1^4 a_2^4}{(a_1^2 + a_2^2)^2}$}}\left\|\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\bm_2}{a_2^2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\bm_1}{a_1^2}$}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\;-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2}$}}\underbrace{\Bigg(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bm_2 \|_2^2}{a_2^2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bm_1 \|_2^2}{a_1^2}$}}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{a_1^2 a_2^2}{a_1^2 + a_2^2}$}}\left\|\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\bm_2}{a_2^2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\bm_1}{a_1^2}$}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\Bigg)}_{\eqqcolon T^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{a_1^2 + a_2^2}{2 a_1^2 a_2^2}$}}\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{a_1^2 a_2^2}{a_1^2 + a_2^2}$}}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\bm_1}{a_1^2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\bm_2}{a_2^2}$}}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2(a_1^2 + a_2^2)}$}}\|\mathbf{m}_{1}-\mathbf{m}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}\;,

where we have simplified
T^{\prime}
as

\displaystyle T^{\prime}\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bm_2 \|_2^2}{a_2^2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bm_1 \|_2^2}{a_1^2}$}}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{a_1^2}{a_2^2( a_1^2 + a_2^2)}$}}\|\mathbf{m}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{a_2^2}{a_1^2( a_1^2 + a_2^2)}$}}\|\mathbf{m}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2}{a_1^2 + a_2^2}$}}\mathbf{m}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{m}_{2}

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{a_1^2 + a_2^2}$}}\|\mathbf{m}_{1}-\mathbf{m}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}\;.

Substituting this into
\mathbb{E}\left[f(\mathbf{X})\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2a_{2}^{2}}\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{m}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\right]
, we have

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\left[f(\mathbf{X})\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2a_2^2}$}}\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{m}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{(2 \pi)^{d/2} a_1^d}$}}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{ \| \bm_1 - \bm_2 \|_2^2}{2 (a_1^2 + a_2^2)}$}}\right)\int f(x)\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{a_1^2 + a_2^2}{2 a_1^2 a_2^2}$}}\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{a_1^2 a_2^2}{a_1^2 + a_2^2}$}}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\bm_1}{a_1^2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\bm_2}{a_2^2}$}}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)dx

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{a_2^2}{a_1^2 + a_2^2}$}}\right)^{d/2}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \bm_1 - \bm_2 \|_2^2}{2 (a_1^2 + a_2^2)}$}}\right)\mathbb{E}[f(\mathbf{W})]\;,

where
\mathbf{W}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\frac{a_{1}^{2}a_{2}^{2}}{a_{1}^{2}+a_{2}^{2}}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\bm_1}{a_1^2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\bm_2}{a_2^2}$}}\right),\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{a_1^2 a_2^2}{a_1^2 + a_2^2}$}}I_{d}\right)
, which completes the proof.

\jmlrQED

Proof of Lemma
29

Rewriting by the tower rule,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[

\displaystyle f(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{X}^{\prime})\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2a_3^2}$}}\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{X}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}\Big)\Big]\;=\;\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[f(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{X}^{\prime})\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2a_3^2}$}}\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{X}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\bigg|\mathbf{X}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{a_3^2}{a_2^2 + a_3^2}$}}\right)^{d/2}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2(a_2^2 + a_3^2)}$}}\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{m}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\mathbb{E}\Big[f\Big(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{W}^{\prime}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{a_2^2 }{a_2^2+a_3^2}$}}\mathbf{X}\Big)\Big|\mathbf{X}\Big]\right]\;,

where the last line follows by applying Lemma
28
to the inner expectation, and where
\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\frac{a_{3}^{2}}{a_{2}^{2}+a_{3}^{2}}\mathbf{m}_{2},\frac{a_{2}^{2}a_{3}^{2}}{a_{2}^{2}+a_{3}^{2}}I_{d}\right)
. Applying Lemma
28
again gives

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\Big[f(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{X}^{\prime})\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2a_3^2}$}}\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{X}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{a_3^2}{a_2^2 + a_3^2}$}}\right)^{d/2}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{a_2^2 + a_3^2}{a_1^2 + a_2^2 + a_3^2}$}}\right)^{d/2}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2(a_1^2 + a_2^2 + a_3^2)}$}}\|\mathbf{m}_{1}-\mathbf{m}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\;\qquad\times\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathbb{E}\Big[f(\mathbf{W},\mathbf{W}^{\prime}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{a_2^2 }{a_2^2+a_3^2}$}}\mathbf{W})\Big|\mathbf{W}\Big]\Big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{a_3^2}{a_1^2 + a_2^2 + a_3^2}$}}\right)^{d/2}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2(a_1^2 + a_2^2 + a_3^2)}$}}\|\mathbf{m}_{1}-\mathbf{m}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\mathbb{E}\Big[f\Big(\mathbf{W},\mathbf{W}^{\prime}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{a_2^2 }{a_2^2+a_3^2}$}}\mathbf{W}\Big)\Big]\;,

where
\mathbf{W}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{a_1^2(a_2^2 + a_3^2)}{a_1^2 + a_2^2 + a_3^2}$}}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{a_1^2}$}}\mathbf{m}_{1}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{a_2^2 + a_3^2}$}}\mathbf{m}_{2}\right),\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{a_1^2(a_2^2 + a_3^2)}{a_1^2 + a_2^2 + a_3^2}$}}I_{d}\right)
.

\jmlrQED

G.2
Proofs for
Section

B.2

Proof of Lemma
30

Consider the sequence of sigma algebras with
\mathcal{F}_{0}
being the trivial sigma algebra and
\mathcal{F}_{i}:=\sigma(X_{1},\ldots,X_{i})
for
i=1,\ldots,n
. This allows us to define a martingale difference sequence: For
i=1,\ldots,n
, let

\displaystyle Y_{i}\;:=\;\mathbb{E}[D_{n}|\mathcal{F}_{i}]-\mathbb{E}[D_{n}|\mathcal{F}_{i-1}]\;.

This implies that
\mathbb{E}[|D_{n}-\mathbb{E}D_{n}|^{\nu}]=\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\sum_{i=1}^{n}Y_{i}\big|^{\nu}\big]
. By Lemma
26
, we get that for some universal constants
c^{\prime}_{\nu},C^{\prime}_{\nu}
,

\displaystyle c^{\prime}_{\nu}\,\msum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[|Y_{i}|^{\nu}]\;\leq\;\mathbb{E}[|D_{n}-\mathbb{E}D_{n}|^{\nu}]\;\leq\;C^{\prime}_{\nu}\,n^{\nu/2-1}\msum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[|Y_{i}|^{\nu}]\;.

(41)

To compute the
\nu
-th moment of
Y_{i}
, recall that
D_{n}=\frac{1}{n(n-1)}\sum_{j,l\in[n],j\neq l}u(\mathbf{X}_{j},\mathbf{X}_{l})
, which implies

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[|Y_{i}|^{\nu}]\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\mathbb{E}[D_{n}|\mathcal{F}_{i}]-\mathbb{E}[D_{n}|\mathcal{F}_{i-1}]\big|^{\nu}\big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n^\nu(n-1)^\nu}$}}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|\msum_{j,l\in[n],j\neq l}\big(\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{j},\mathbf{X}_{l})|\mathcal{F}_{i}]-\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{j},\mathbf{X}_{l})|\mathcal{F}_{i-1}]\big)\big|^{\nu}\Big]

\displaystyle\;\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2}{n^\nu(n-1)^\nu}$}}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|\msum_{j\in[n],j\neq i}\big(\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{i},\mathbf{X}_{j})|\mathcal{F}_{i}]-\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{i},\mathbf{X}_{j})|\mathcal{F}_{i-1}]\big)\big|^{\nu}\Big]

\displaystyle\;=:

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2}{n^\nu(n-1)^\nu}$}}\,\mathbb{E}[|S_{i}|^{\nu}]\;.

In
(a)
, we have used that each summand is zero if both
j
and
l
do not equal
i
, and that
u
is symmetric. In the case
j<i
, we can compute each summand of
S_{i}
as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{i},\mathbf{X}_{j})|\mathcal{F}_{i}]-\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{i},\mathbf{X}_{j})|\mathcal{F}_{i-1}]\;=

\displaystyle\;u(\mathbf{X}_{i},\mathbf{X}_{j})-\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{j})|\mathbf{X}_{j}]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;A_{ij}-B_{j}+B_{i}\;,

where
A_{ij}:=u(\mathbf{X}_{i},\mathbf{X}_{j})-\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{i},\mathbf{X}_{1})|\mathbf{X}_{i}]
and

\displaystyle B_{i}\;:=\;\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{i},\mathbf{X}_{1})|\mathbf{X}_{i}]-\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})]\;=\;\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{i})|\mathbf{X}_{i}]-\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})]\;

by symmetry of
u
. In the case
j>i
, we can compute each summand as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{i},\mathbf{X}_{j})|\mathcal{F}_{i}]-\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{i},\mathbf{X}_{j})|\mathcal{F}_{i-1}]\;=\;\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{i})|\mathbf{X}_{i}]-\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})]\;=\;B_{i}\;.

Therefore

\displaystyle S_{i}\;=

\displaystyle\;\msum_{j<i}(A_{ij}-B_{j})+nB_{i}\;.

Consider
R_{1}:=nB_{i}
and
R_{2}:=\sum_{j<i}(A_{ij}-B_{j})
, which forms a two-element martingale difference sequence with respect to the filtration
\sigma(\mathbf{X}_{i})\subseteq\sigma(\mathbf{X}_{i},\mathbf{X}_{1}\ldots,\mathbf{X}_{i-1})
. By Lemma
26
again, there exist constants
c^{*}_{\nu}
and
C^{*}_{\nu}
depending only on
\nu
such that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[|S_{i}|^{\nu}]\;=\;\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\msum_{l=1}^{2}R_{l}\big|^{\nu}\big]\;

\displaystyle\leq\;C^{*}_{\nu}\Big(\mathbb{E}[|nB_{i}|^{\nu}]+\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\msum_{j<i}(A_{ij}-B_{j})\big|^{\nu}\big]\Big)

\displaystyle=\;C^{*}_{\nu}\big(n^{\nu}M_{\rm cond;\nu}^{\nu}+\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\msum_{j<i}(A_{ij}-B_{j})\big|^{\nu}\big]\big)\;,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[|S_{i}|^{\nu}]\;=\;\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\msum_{l=1}^{2}R_{l}\big|^{\nu}\big]\;

\displaystyle\geq\;c^{*}_{\nu}\Big(\mathbb{E}[|nB_{i}|^{\nu}]+\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\msum_{j<i}(A_{ij}-B_{j})\big|^{\nu}\big]\Big)

\displaystyle=\;c^{*}_{\nu}\big(n^{\nu}M_{\rm cond;\nu}^{\nu}+\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\msum_{j<i}(A_{ij}-B_{j})\big|^{\nu}\big]\big)\;.

Now consider
T_{j}:=A_{ij}-B_{j}
for
j=1,\ldots,i-1
, which again forms a martingale difference sequence with respect to
\sigma(\mathbf{X}_{i},\mathbf{X}_{1}),\ldots,\sigma(\mathbf{X}_{i},\mathbf{X}_{1}\ldots,\mathbf{X}_{i-1})
. Then by Lemma
26
again, there exist constants
c^{\Delta}_{\nu}
and
C^{\Delta}_{\nu}
depending only on
\nu
such that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\msum_{j<i}(A_{ij}-B_{j})\big|^{\nu}\big]\;

\displaystyle\leq\;C^{\Delta}_{\nu}\,(i-1)^{\nu/2-1}\msum_{j=1}^{i-1}\mathbb{E}[|A_{ij}-B_{j}|^{\nu}]\;=\;C^{\Delta}_{\nu}\,(i-1)^{\nu/2}M_{\rm full;\nu}^{\nu}\;,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\msum_{j<i}(A_{ij}-B_{j})\big|^{\nu}\big]\;

\displaystyle\geq\;c^{\Delta}_{\nu}\,\msum_{j=1}^{i-1}\mathbb{E}[|A_{ij}-B_{j}|^{\nu}]\;=\;c^{\Delta}_{\nu}\,(i-1)M_{\rm full;\nu}^{\nu}\;.

Therefore

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[|S_{i}|^{\nu}]\;\leq

\displaystyle\;C^{*}_{\nu}n^{\nu}M_{\rm cond;\nu}^{\nu}+C^{*}_{\nu}C^{\Delta}_{\nu}\,(i-1)^{\nu/2}M_{\rm full;\nu}^{\nu}\;,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[|S_{i}|^{\nu}]\;\geq

\displaystyle\;c^{*}_{\nu}n^{\nu}M_{\rm cond;\nu}^{\nu}+c^{*}_{\nu}c^{\Delta}_{\nu}\,(i-1)M_{\rm full;\nu}^{\nu}\;,

which yield the following bounds on the
\nu
-th moment of
Y_{i}
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[|Y_{i}|^{\nu}]\;

\displaystyle\leq\;2C^{*}_{\nu}\big((n-1)^{-\nu}M_{\rm cond;\nu}^{\nu}+C^{\Delta}_{\nu}n^{-\nu}(n-1)^{-\nu}\,(i-1)^{\nu/2}M_{\rm full;\nu}^{\nu}\big)\;,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[|Y_{i}|^{\nu}]\;

\displaystyle\geq\;2c^{*}_{\nu}\big((n-1)^{-\nu}M_{\rm cond;\nu}^{\nu}+c^{\Delta}_{\nu}n^{-\nu}(n-1)^{-\nu}\,(i-1)M_{\rm full;\nu}^{\nu}\big)\;,

To sum these terms over
i=1,\ldots,n
, we note that since
\nu/2>0
,

\displaystyle\msum_{i=1}^{n}(i-1)^{\nu/2}\;\leq\;\mint_{0}^{n}x^{\nu/2}dx\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{n^{1+\nu/2}}{1+\nu/2}$}}\;,

\displaystyle\msum_{i=1}^{n}(i-1)\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{n(n-1)}{2}$}}\;.

Define
C_{\nu}:=\frac{2C^{\prime}_{\nu}C^{*}_{\nu}\max\nolimits\{1,C^{\Delta}_{\nu}\}}{1+\nu/2}
and
c_{\nu}:=c^{\prime}_{\nu}c^{*}_{\nu}\min\{1,c^{\Delta}_{\nu}\}
. By summing the bounds on
\mathbb{E}[|Y_{i}|^{\nu}]
and substituting into (
41
), we get the desired bounds

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[|D_{n}-\mathbb{E}D_{n}|^{\nu}]\;

\displaystyle\leq\;C_{\nu}\,n^{\nu/2-1}\,\big(n(n-1)^{-\nu}M_{\rm cond;\nu}^{\nu}+n^{-\nu}(n-1)^{-\nu}n^{1+\nu/2}M_{\rm full;\nu}^{\nu}\big)

\displaystyle=\;C_{\nu}\,n^{\nu/2}(n-1)^{-\nu}M_{\rm cond;\nu}^{\nu}+C_{\nu}\,(n-1)^{-\nu}M_{\rm full;\nu}^{\nu}\;,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[|D_{n}-\mathbb{E}D_{n}|^{\nu}]\;

\displaystyle\geq\;c_{\nu}n(n-1)^{-\nu}M_{\rm cond;\nu}^{\nu}+c_{\nu}n^{-(\nu-1)}(n-1)^{-(\nu-1)}M_{\rm full;\nu}^{\nu}\;.

\jmlrQED

Proof of Lemma
31

The first result is directly obtained from linearity of expectation and Jensen’s inequality:

\displaystyle\Big|D-\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}\mu_{k}^{2}\Big|\;=

\displaystyle\;\Big|\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})]-\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}\mathbb{E}[\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})]\mathbb{E}[\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})]\Big|

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\Big|\mathbb{E}\Big[u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})-\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})\Big]\Big|

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\Big|u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})-\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})\Big|\;=\;\varepsilon_{K;1}\;.

To prove the next few bounds, we first derive a useful inequality: For
a,b\in\mathbb{R}
and
\nu\geq 1
, by Jensen’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle|a+b|^{\nu}\;=\;\big|\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2}$}}(2a)+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2}$}}(2b)\big|^{\nu}\;\leq\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2}$}}|2a|^{\nu}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2}$}}|2b|^{\nu}\;=\;2^{\nu-1}(|a|^{\nu}+|b|^{\nu})\;.

By a triangle inequality followed by applying the above inequality again with
a
replaced by
|a|-|b|
and
b
replaced by
|b|
, we have

\displaystyle|a+b|^{\nu}\;\geq\;||a|-|b||^{\nu}\;\geq\;2^{-(\nu-1)}|a|^{\nu}-|b|^{\nu}\;.

Since
\nu\in[1,3]
, we have
2^{\nu-1}\in[1,4]
. Therefore

\displaystyle\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{4}$}}|a|^{\nu}-|b|^{\nu}\;\leq\;|a+b|^{\nu}\;\leq\;4(|a|^{\nu}+|b|^{\nu})\;.

(42)

Now to prove the conditional bound, we make use of the fact that
\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2}
are i.i.d. to see that

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big|\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}(\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})-\mu_{k})\mu_{k}\Big|^{\nu}\Big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big|\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}\big(\mathbb{E}[\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})|\mathbf{X}_{1}]-\mathbb{E}[\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})]\big)\Big|^{\nu}\Big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big|\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})|\mathbf{X}_{1}]-\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})]+\Delta_{K;1}-\Delta_{K;2}\Big|^{\nu}\Big]\;,

(43)

where

\displaystyle\Delta_{K;1}\;\coloneqq

\displaystyle\;\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}\mathbb{E}[\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})|\mathbf{X}_{1}]-\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})|\mathbf{X}_{1}]\;,

\displaystyle\Delta_{K;2}\;\coloneqq

\displaystyle\;\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}\mathbb{E}[\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})]-\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})]\;.

Moments of the two error terms can be bounded by Jensen’s inequality applied to
x\mapsto|x|^{\nu}
with respect to the conditional expectation
\mathbb{E}[{\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,}|\mathbf{X}_{2}]
and the expectation
\mathbb{E}[{\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,}]
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[|\Delta_{K;1}|^{\nu}],\mathbb{E}[|\Delta_{K;2}|^{\nu}]\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big|u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})-\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})\Big|^{\nu}\Big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\Big\|u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})-\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})\Big\|_{L_{\nu}}^{\nu}\;=\;\varepsilon_{K;\nu}^{\nu}\;.

On the other hand,

\displaystyle(M_{\rm cond;\nu})^{\nu}\;=\;\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})|\mathbf{X}_{1}]-\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})]\big|^{\nu}\big]\;.

Therefore applying (
42
) gives

\displaystyle\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{4}$}}(M_{\rm cond;\nu})^{\nu}-\varepsilon_{K;\nu}^{\nu}\;\leq\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big|\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}(\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})-\mu_{k})\mu_{k}\Big|^{\nu}\Big]\;\leq\;4((M_{\rm cond;\nu})^{\nu}+\varepsilon_{K;\nu}^{\nu})

For the last bound, we start by considering the following quantity, which can be thought of as the truncated version of
M_{\rm full;\nu}^{\nu}
:

\displaystyle m_{K}\;\coloneqq

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big|\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}(\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})-\mu_{k}^{2})\Big|^{\nu}\Big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big|\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}(\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})-\mu_{k})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})+\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}\mu_{k}(\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})-\mu_{k})\Big|^{\nu}\Big]

\displaystyle\;\eqqcolon

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}[|T_{2}+T_{1}|^{\nu}]\;.

Since
\{T_{1},T_{2}\}
forms a two-element martingale difference sequence with respect to
\sigma(\mathbf{X}_{2})\subseteq\sigma(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})
, by Lemma
26
, there exists absolute constants
c^{\prime}_{\nu},C^{\prime}_{\nu}>0
depending only on
\nu
such that

\displaystyle c^{\prime}_{\nu}\big(\mathbb{E}[|T_{1}|^{\nu}]+\mathbb{E}[|T_{2}|^{\nu}]\big)\;\leq\;m_{K}\;\leq\;C^{\prime}_{\nu}\big(\mathbb{E}[|T_{1}|^{\nu}]+\mathbb{E}[|T_{2}|^{\nu}]\big)\;.

Similarly, by writing

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[|T_{2}|^{\nu}]\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big|\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}(\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})-\mu_{k})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})\Big|^{\nu}\Big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big|\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}(\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})-\mu_{k})(\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})-\mu_{k})+\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}(\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})-\mu_{k})\mu_{k}\Big|^{\nu}\Big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}[|R_{2}+R_{1}|^{\nu}]\;,

and noting that
\{R_{1},R_{2}\}
forms a two-element martingale difference sequence with respect to
\sigma(\mathbf{X}_{1})\subseteq\sigma(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})
, by Lemma
26
, there exists absolute constants
c^{\prime\prime}_{\nu},C^{\prime\prime}_{\nu}>0
depending only on
\nu
such that

\displaystyle c^{\prime\prime}_{\nu}\big(\mathbb{E}[|R_{1}|^{\nu}]+\mathbb{E}[|R_{2}|^{\nu}]\big)\;\leq\;\mathbb{E}[|T_{2}|^{\nu}]\;\leq\;C^{\prime\prime}_{\nu}\big(\mathbb{E}[|R_{1}|^{\nu}]+\mathbb{E}[|R_{2}|^{\nu}]\big)\;.

Combining the results and setting
A=\sup\nolimits_{\nu\in[1,3]}C^{\prime}_{\nu}\max\{C^{\prime\prime}_{\nu},1\}
and
a=\inf\nolimits_{\nu\in[1,3]}c^{\prime}_{\nu}\min\{c^{\prime\prime}_{\nu},1\}
, we have shown that

\displaystyle a\big(\mathbb{E}[|T_{1}|^{\nu}]+\mathbb{E}[|R_{1}|^{\nu}]+\mathbb{E}[|R_{2}|^{\nu}]\big)\;\leq\;m_{K}\;\leq

\displaystyle\;A\big(\mathbb{E}[|T_{1}|^{\nu}]+\mathbb{E}[|R_{1}|^{\nu}]+\mathbb{E}[|R_{2}|^{\nu}]\big)\;.

Notice that the quantity we would like to control is exactly

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[|R_{2}|^{\nu}]\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big|\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}(\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})-\mu_{k})(\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})-\mu_{k})\Big|^{\nu}\Big]\;,

and that
\mathbb{E}[|T_{1}|^{\nu}]=\mathbb{E}[|R_{1}|^{\nu}]
. By setting
c=A^{-1}
and
C=a^{-1}
, this allows us to obtain a bound about
\mathbb{E}[|R_{2}|^{\nu}]
as

\displaystyle cm_{K}-2\mathbb{E}[|T_{1}|^{\nu}]\leq\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big|\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}(\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})-\mu_{k})(\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})-\mu_{k})\Big|^{\nu}\Big]\leq Cm_{K}-2\mathbb{E}[|T_{1}|^{\nu}]\;.

Now notice that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[|T_{1}|^{\nu}]\;=\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big|\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}(\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})-\mu_{k})\mu_{k}\Big|^{\nu}\Big]\;,

which has already been controlled by the second result of the lemma as

\displaystyle\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{4}$}}(M_{\rm cond;\nu})^{\nu}-\varepsilon_{K;\nu}^{\nu}\;\leq\;\mathbb{E}[|T_{1}|^{\nu}]\;\leq\;4((M_{\rm cond;\nu})^{\nu}+\varepsilon_{K;\nu}^{\nu})\;.

On the other hand, we can use an exactly analogous argument by using (
42
) and applying Jensen’s inequality to control the errors to show that

\displaystyle\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{4}$}}(M_{\rm full;\nu})^{\nu}-\varepsilon_{K;\nu}^{\nu}\;\leq\;m_{K}\;\leq\;4((M_{\rm full;\nu})^{\nu}+\varepsilon_{K;\nu}^{\nu})\;.

Applying these two results to the previous bound gives the desired bounds:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big|\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}(\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})-\mu_{k})(\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})-\mu_{k})\Big|^{\nu}\Big]\leq

\displaystyle\;4C(M_{\rm full;\nu})^{\nu}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2}$}}(M_{\rm cond;\nu})^{\nu}+(4C+2)\varepsilon_{K;\nu}^{\nu}\;,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big|\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}(\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})-\mu_{k})(\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})-\mu_{k})\Big|^{\nu}\Big]\geq

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{c}{4}$}}(M_{\rm full;\nu})^{\nu}-8(M_{\rm cond;\nu})^{\nu}-(c+8)\varepsilon_{K;\nu}^{\nu}\;.

\jmlrQED

Proof of Lemma
32

To compute the first bound, we rewrite the expression of interest as a quantity that we have already considered in the proof of Lemma
31
:

\displaystyle(\mu^{K})^{\top}\Lambda^{K}\Sigma^{K}\Lambda^{K}(\mu^{K})\;=

\displaystyle\;(\mu^{K})^{\top}\Lambda^{K}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big(\phi^{K}(\mathbf{X}_{1})-\mu^{K}\big)\big(\phi^{K}(\mathbf{X}_{1})-\mu^{K}\big)^{\top}\Big]\Lambda^{K}(\mu^{K})

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(\big(\phi^{K}(\mathbf{X}_{1})-\mu^{K}\big)^{\top}\Lambda^{K}\mu^{K}\Big)^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}(\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})-\mu_{k})\mu_{k}\Big)^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})|\mathbf{X}_{2}]-\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})]+\Delta_{K;1}-\Delta_{K;2}\Big)^{2}\Big]\;,

where we have used the calculation in (
43
) with
\nu=2
and defined the same error terms

\displaystyle\Delta_{K;1}\;\coloneqq

\displaystyle\;\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}\mathbb{E}[\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})|\mathbf{X}_{2}]-\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})|\mathbf{X}_{2}]\;,

\displaystyle\Delta_{K;2}\;\coloneqq

\displaystyle\;\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}\mathbb{E}[\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})]-\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})]\;.

Since we are dealing with the second moment, we can provide a finer bound by expanding the square explicitly:

\displaystyle(\mu^{K})^{\top}\Lambda^{K}\Sigma^{K}\Lambda^{K}(\mu^{K})\;=

\displaystyle\;\text{\rm Var}\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})|\mathbf{X}_{2}]+\mathbb{E}[(\Delta_{K;1}-\Delta_{K;2})^{2}]

\displaystyle\;+2\mathbb{E}\big[\big(\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})|\mathbf{X}_{2}]-\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})]\big)(\Delta_{K;1}-\Delta_{K;2})^{2}\big]\;.

Then by a Cauchy-Schwartz inequality, we get that

\displaystyle\;\Big|(\mu^{K})^{\top}\Lambda^{K}\Sigma^{K}\Lambda^{K}(\mu^{K})-\text{\rm Var}\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})|\mathbf{X}_{2}]\Big|

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;2\big|\mathbb{E}\big[\big(\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})|\mathbf{X}_{2}]-\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})]\big)(\Delta_{K;1}-\Delta_{K;2})^{2}\big]\big|+\mathbb{E}[(\Delta_{K;1}-\Delta_{K;2})^{2}]

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;2\sqrt{\text{\rm Var}\mathbb{E}[u(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})|\mathbf{X}_{2}]}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[(\Delta_{K;1}-\Delta_{K;2})^{2}]}+\mathbb{E}[(\Delta_{K;1}-\Delta_{K;2})^{2}]\;.

The variance term is exactly
\sigma_{\rm cond}^{2}
. Since the individual error terms have already been bounded in the proof of Lemma
31
as
\mathbb{E}[\Delta_{K;1}^{2}],\mathbb{E}[\Delta_{K;2}^{2}]\leq\varepsilon_{K;2}^{2}
, by a triangle inequality and a Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}[(\Delta_{K;1}-\Delta_{K;2})^{2}]|\;=

\displaystyle\;|\mathbb{E}[\Delta_{K;1}^{2}]-2\mathbb{E}[\Delta_{K;1}\Delta_{K;2}]+\mathbb{E}[\Delta_{K;2}^{2}]|

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;|\mathbb{E}[\Delta_{K;1}^{2}]|+2\sqrt{|\mathbb{E}[\Delta_{K;1}^{2}]||\mathbb{E}[\Delta_{K;2}^{2}]|}+|\mathbb{E}[\Delta_{K;2}^{2}]|\;\leq\;4\varepsilon_{K;2}^{2}\;.

Combining the bounds gives

\displaystyle\big|(\mu^{K})^{\top}\Lambda^{K}\Sigma^{K}\Lambda^{K}(\mu^{K})-(\sigma_{\rm cond})^{2}\big|\;\leq\;4\varepsilon_{K;2}^{2}+4\sigma_{\rm cond}\varepsilon_{K;2}\;,

which rearranges to give

\displaystyle\sigma_{\rm cond}^{2}-4\sigma_{\rm cond}\varepsilon_{K;2}-4\varepsilon_{K;2}^{2}\;\leq\;(\mu^{K})^{\top}\Lambda^{K}\Sigma^{K}\Lambda^{K}(\mu^{K})\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\sigma_{\rm cond}^{2}+4\sigma_{\rm cond}\varepsilon_{K;2}+4\varepsilon_{K;2}^{2}

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;(\sigma_{\rm cond}+2\varepsilon_{K;2})^{2}\;.

The second bound is obtained similarly by giving a finer control than the bound in Lemma
31
. We first rewrite the expression of interest by using linearity of expectation and the cyclic property of trace:

\displaystyle\text{\rm Tr}((\Lambda^{K}\Sigma^{K})^{2})\;=

\displaystyle\;\text{\rm Tr}\big(\Lambda^{K}\mathbb{E}\big[\phi^{K}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi^{K}(\mathbf{X}_{1})^{\top}\big]\Lambda^{K}\mathbb{E}\big[\phi^{K}(\mathbf{X}_{2})\phi^{K}(\mathbf{X}_{2})^{\top}\big]\big)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\big(\phi^{K}(\mathbf{X}_{1})^{\top}\Lambda^{K}\phi^{K}(\mathbf{X}_{2})\big)^{2}\Big]\;=\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})\Big)^{2}\Big]\;.

Again by expanding the square explicitly, we get that

\displaystyle\text{\rm Tr}((\Lambda^{K}\Sigma^{K})^{2})\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})-\bar{u}(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})+\bar{u}(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})\Big)^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})-\bar{u}(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})\Big)^{2}\Big]+\mathbb{E}\big[\bar{u}(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})^{2}\big]+2\Delta_{K;3}

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\varepsilon_{K;2}^{2}+\sigma_{\rm full}^{2}+2\Delta_{K;3}\;,

where we have defined the additional error term as

\displaystyle\Delta_{K;3}\;\coloneqq\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})-\bar{u}(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})\Big)\bar{u}(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})\Big]\;.

By a Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we get that

\displaystyle\big|\text{\rm Tr}(

\displaystyle(\Lambda^{K}\Sigma^{K})^{2})-\sigma_{\rm full}^{2}-\varepsilon_{K;2}^{2}\big|\;=\;2|\Delta_{K;3}|

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;2\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\big[\big(\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})-\bar{u}(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})\big)^{2}\big]}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\big[\bar{u}(\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2})^{2}\big]}\;=\;2\varepsilon_{K;2}\sigma_{\rm full}\;.

Combining the above two bounds yields the desired inequality that

\displaystyle(\sigma_{\rm full}-\varepsilon_{K;2})^{2}\;\leq\;\text{\rm Tr}((\Lambda^{K}\Sigma^{K})^{2})\;\leq\;(\sigma_{\rm full}+\varepsilon_{K;2})^{2}\;.

To prove the third bound, note that
(\mu^{K})^{\top}\Lambda^{K}\mathbf{Z}_{1}
is a zero-mean normal random variable with variance given by
(\mu^{K})^{\top}\Lambda^{K}\Sigma^{K}\mu^{K}
, which is already bounded above. By applying the formula of the
\nu
-th absolute moment of a normal distribution and noting that
\nu\leq 3
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[|(\mu^{K})^{\top}

\displaystyle\Lambda^{K}\mathbf{Z}_{1}|^{\nu}]\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2^{\nu/2}}{\sqrt{\pi}}$}}\Gamma\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\nu+1}{2}$}}\Big)\big((\mu^{K})^{\top}\Lambda^{K}\Sigma^{K}\Lambda^{K}\mu^{K}\big)^{\nu/2}

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2^{\nu/2}}{\sqrt{\pi}}$}}(\sigma_{\rm full}+2\varepsilon_{K;2})^{\nu}\;\overset{(a)}{\leq}\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2^{\nu/2}}{\sqrt{\pi}}$}}\max\nolimits\{1,2^{\nu-1}\}\big(\sigma_{\rm cond}^{\nu}+2^{\nu}\varepsilon_{K;2}^{\nu}\big)\;\overset{(b)}{\leq}\;7(\sigma_{\rm cond}^{\nu}+8\varepsilon_{K;2}^{\nu})\;.

In
(a)
, we have noted that given
a,b>0
, for
\nu/2\in(0,1]
,
(a+b)^{\nu/2}\leq a^{\nu/2}+b^{\nu/2}
and for
\nu/2>1
, the bound follows from Jensen’s inequality. In
(b)
, we have noted that
\nu\leq 3
. This finishes the proof for the third bound.

To prove the fourth bound, we can first condition on
\mathbf{Z}_{2}
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[|\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}\Lambda^{K}\mathbf{Z}_{2}|^{\nu}]\;=\;\mathbb{E}\big[\,\mathbb{E}[|\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}\Lambda^{K}\mathbf{Z}_{2}|^{\nu}|\,\mathbf{Z}_{2}]\,\big]\;.

The inner expectation is again the
\nu
-th absolute moment of a conditionally Gaussian random variable with variance
\mathbf{Z}_{2}^{\top}\Lambda^{K}\Sigma^{K}\Lambda^{K}\mathbf{Z}_{2}
, so again by the formula of the
\nu
-th absolute moment of a normal distribution, we get that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[|\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}\Lambda^{K}\mathbf{Z}_{2}|^{\nu}]\;\leq\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2^{\nu/2}}{\sqrt{\pi}}$}}\,\mathbb{E}\Big[\big(\mathbf{Z}_{2}^{\top}\Lambda^{K}\Sigma^{K}\Lambda^{K}\mathbf{Z}_{2}\big)^{\nu/2}\Big]\;\leq\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2^{\nu/2}}{\sqrt{\pi}}$}}\,\mathbb{E}\Big[\big(\mathbf{Z}_{2}^{\top}\Lambda^{K}\Sigma^{K}\Lambda^{K}\mathbf{Z}_{2}\big)^{2}\Big]^{\nu/4}\;.

We have noted that
\nu\leq 3
and used a Hölder’s inequality. The remaining expectation is taken over a quadratic form of normal variables. Writing
\Sigma_{*}=(\Sigma^{K})^{1/2}\Lambda^{K}(\Sigma^{K})^{1/2}
for short, the second moment can be computed by the formula from Lemma
27
as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\big(\mathbf{Z}_{2}^{\top}\Lambda^{K}\Sigma^{K}\Lambda^{K}\mathbf{Z}_{2}\big)^{2}\Big]\;=

\displaystyle\;\text{\rm Tr}(\Sigma_{*}^{2})^{2}+2\text{\rm Tr}\big(\Sigma_{*}^{4}\big)\;\overset{(a)}{\leq}\;3\text{\rm Tr}(\Sigma_{*}^{2})^{2}\;=\;3\text{\rm Tr}\big((\Lambda^{K}\Sigma^{K})^{2}\big)^{2}\;.

Note that in
(a)
, we have used the fact that the square of a symmetric matrix,
\Sigma_{*}^{2}
, has non-negative eigenvalues, and therefore
\text{\rm Tr}(\Sigma_{*}^{4})\leq\text{\rm Tr}(\Sigma_{*}^{2})^{2}
. Since we have already bounded
\text{\rm Tr}((\Lambda^{K}\Sigma^{K})^{2})
earlier, substituting the above result into the previous bound, we get that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[|\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}\Lambda^{K}\mathbf{Z}_{2}|^{\nu}]\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2^{\nu/2}}{\sqrt{\pi}}$}}\,\mathbb{E}\Big[\big(\mathbf{Z}_{2}^{\top}\Lambda^{K}\Sigma^{K}\Lambda^{K}\mathbf{Z}_{2}\big)^{2}\Big]^{\nu/4}\leq\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2^{\nu/2} 3^{\nu/4}}{\sqrt{\pi}}$}}\,\text{\rm Tr}\big((\Lambda^{K}\Sigma^{K})^{2}\big)^{\nu/2}

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2^{\nu/2} 3^{\nu/4}}{\sqrt{\pi}}$}}(\sigma_{\rm full}^{2}+\varepsilon_{K;2}^{2})^{\nu/2}

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2^{\nu/2} 3^{\nu/4}}{\sqrt{\pi}}$}}\max\nolimits\{1,2^{\nu/2-1}\}\big(\sigma_{\rm full}^{\nu}+\varepsilon_{K;2}^{\nu}\big)\;\leq\;6\big(\sigma_{\rm full}^{\nu}+\varepsilon_{K;2}^{\nu}\big)\;.

In the last two inequalities, we have used the same argument as in the proof for the third bound to expand the term with
\nu
-th power. This gives the desired bound.

To prove the final bound, we first condition on
\mathbf{X}_{1}
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\big|(\phi^{K}(\mathbf{X}_{1})-\mu^{K})^{\top}\Lambda^{K}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\big|^{\nu}\big]\;=\;\mathbb{E}\big[\mathbb{E}\big[\big|(\phi^{K}(\mathbf{X}_{1})-\mu^{K})^{\top}\Lambda^{K}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\big|^{\nu}\big|\mathbf{X}_{1}\big]\big]\;.

The inner expectation is the
\nu
-th absolute moment of a conditionally Gaussian random variable with variance
(\phi^{K}(\mathbf{X}_{1})-\mu^{K})^{\top}\Lambda^{K}\Sigma^{K}\Lambda^{K}(\phi^{K}(\mathbf{X}_{1})-\mu^{K})
, so by the formula of the
\nu
-th absolute moment of a normal distribution with
\nu\leq 3
, we get that

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}[|(\phi^{K}(\mathbf{X}_{1})-\mu^{K})^{\top}\Lambda^{K}\mathbf{Z}_{2}|^{\nu}]

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2^{\nu/2}}{\sqrt{\pi}}$}}\,\mathbb{E}\Big[\big((\phi^{K}(\mathbf{X}_{1})-\mu^{K})^{\top}\Lambda^{K}\Sigma^{K}\Lambda^{K}(\phi^{K}(\mathbf{X}_{1})-\mu^{K})\big)^{\nu/2}\Big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2^{\nu/2}}{\sqrt{\pi}}$}}\,\mathbb{E}\Big[\big((\phi^{K}(\mathbf{X}_{1})-\mu^{K})^{\top}\Lambda^{K}\mathbb{E}\big[(\phi^{K}(\mathbf{X}_{2})-\mu^{K})(\phi^{K}(\mathbf{X}_{2})-\mu^{K})^{\top}\big]\Lambda^{K}(\phi^{K}(\mathbf{X}_{1})-\mu^{K})\big)^{\nu/2}\Big]

\displaystyle\;\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2^{\nu/2}}{\sqrt{\pi}}$}}\,\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|(\phi^{K}(\mathbf{X}_{1})-\mu^{K})^{\top}\Lambda^{K}(\phi^{K}(\mathbf{X}_{2})-\mu^{K})\big|^{\nu}\Big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2^{\nu/2}}{\sqrt{\pi}}$}}\,\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|\msum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{k}\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{1})\phi_{k}(\mathbf{X}_{2})\big|^{\nu}\Big]

\displaystyle\;\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\;8C(M_{\rm full;\nu})^{\nu}-(M_{\rm cond;\nu})^{\nu}+(8C+4)\varepsilon_{K;\nu}^{\nu}\;.

In
(a)
, we have applied Jensen’s inequality to the convex function
x\mapsto|x|^{\nu/2}
to move the inner expectation outside the norm. In
(b)
, we have applied the bound in Lemma
31
and noted that
\frac{2^{\nu/2}}{\sqrt{\pi}}<2
for
\nu\in[1,3]
. This gives the desired result.

\jmlrQED

Proof of Lemma
33

For the first equality in distribution, we recall that
\{\tau_{k;d}\}_{k=1}^{K}
are the eigenvalues of
(\Sigma^{K})^{1/2}\Lambda^{K}(\Sigma^{K})^{1/2}
and
\{\xi_{k}\}_{k=1}^{K}
are a sequence of i.i.d. standard Gaussian variables. Let
\{\eta_{ik}\}_{i\in[n],k\in[K]}
be a set of i.i.d. standard Gaussian variables. Since Gaussianity is preserved under orthogonal transformation, we have

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n^{3/2}(n-1)^{1/2}}$}}\Big(\msum_{i,j=1}^{n}(\eta^{K}_{i})^{\top}(\Sigma^{K})^{1/2}\Lambda^{K}(\Sigma^{K})^{1/2}\eta^{K}_{j}-n\text{\rm Tr}(\Sigma^{K}\Lambda^{K})\Big)

\displaystyle\;\overset{d}{=}

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n^{3/2}(n-1)^{1/2}}$}}\Big(\msum_{k=1}^{K}\msum_{i,j=1}^{n}\tau_{k;d}\eta_{ik}\eta_{jk}-n\text{\rm Tr}((\Sigma^{K})^{1/2}\Lambda^{K}(\Sigma^{K})^{1/2})\Big)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n^{3/2}(n-1)^{1/2}}$}}\msum_{k=1}^{K}\tau_{k;d}\Big(\big(\msum_{i=1}^{n}\eta_{ik}\big)\big(\msum_{j=1}^{n}\eta_{jk}\big)-n\Big)

\displaystyle\;\overset{d}{=}

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n^{1/2}(n-1)^{1/2}}$}}\msum_{k=1}^{K}\tau_{k;d}(\xi_{k}^{2}-1)\;=\;W_{n}^{K}-D\;,

which proves the desired statement.

We now use the expression above for moment computation. The expectation is given by
\mathbb{E}[W_{n}^{K}]=D
for every
K\in\mathbb{N}
. The variance can be computed by noting that the quantity is a quadratic form in Gaussian, applying Lemma
27
